# Shawn Baker — 决策维度研究 (R01)

## 轮 1/17 (2018-01-01 → 2020-01-23)

### 调研元数据

- 素材范围: `@ShawnBakerMD/20180101001.md` – `@ShawnBakerMD/20200123001.md` (100 个 .md 文件)
- 来源: YouTube auto-caption 直链下载 (`caption_source: youtube_auto_caption_direct_download`), 大多 `review_status: direct_download_unreviewed`
- 调研方向: 公开做出的重大决策 (写书、开诊所、转 carnivore、成立 Meat Heals、运动 etc.) + 个人重大生活事件 (健康转折、运动突破、家庭 etc.) + 与主张强相关的行动

### 关键 grep 命令

| 命令 | 命中文件数 |
|------|----------|
| `grep -iE "I did\|I decided\|I started\|I wrote\|my book\|I quit\|I changed\|I stopped\|I was\|became\|career\|orthopedic\|surgery\|revero\|clinic"` | 71 |
| `grep -iE "personal\|family\|wife\|kid\|life\|story\|journey"` | 75 |
| `grep -iE "orthopedic\|surgery\|surgeon\|surgeries"` | ~10 (核心 1 个文件) |
| `grep -iE "I started eating\|I changed my diet\|stopped eating\|carnivore diet\|my health\|disease"` | 全部 100 (此轮主旋律即 carnivore) |
| `grep -iE "rowing\|world record\|athletic\|row\|run\|ran\|race\|racing\|marathon\|triathlon\|kilimanjaro\|basketball"` | 多文件中含运动/挑战碎片 |
| `grep -iE "my book\|book came\|the carnivore diet book\|publisher\|publish\|meat heals\|meatheals\|founded"` | 多个含 "my book" / "meatheals.com" |
| `grep -iE "I went\|I came\|I moved\|I grew up\|medical school\|residency\|graduated"` | 集中于 20180721001 / 20190222001 / 20190606001 / 20191124001 |
| `grep -iE "I'm a doctor\|I'm an orthopedic\|I'm a trauma\|I'm 51\|I'm 52\|I'm 53\|cattle\|ranch\|bought"` | 提及 rancher 联系、年纪等 |

### 关键发现 (按"决策量级"排序)

#### 1. 医疗执照被州医委会吊销 → 自愿放弃 → 独立评估 → 恢复 (2017-10 弃 → 2017-12 评估 → 2018-04 报告 → 2018-05 通知可重申请 → 2019-02 投票恢复 → 2019-06-03 签发)

**[他/她说过的]** [20180721001.md 00:02:47 – 00:03:39] "had a great career only when i decided that i no longer wanted to to put surgeries on people when rather they could they could benefit from lifestyle that impacted the hospitals bottom line did i get into trouble with the hospital the state board then asked me if i want to surrender my license or go through a lengthy legal battle with them i voluntarily surrendered my license… the state back in may sent me a letter said we would like you to reapply for your license so and i'm in the process of doing that so this ex dr bs is just crap i'll always be a doctor you can call me a non-practicing physician but this you know ex-doctor crap is just silly… i spent 20 years taking care of people and saving a lot of their lives."

**[他/她说过的]** [20190222001.md 00:02:16 – 00:03:00] "i chose that route and so at the latter part of 2017 I went up to a place in Denver that basically does these independent reviews and they reviewed me spent quite a climb doing it it took about four or five months to generate the report the report was from finally generated this past spring that report said I was completely competent to return to practice… today February 21st I was just informed that the State Board had voted to reinstate my license which obviously I'm very happy and very thankful for you know it's been a it's been a very difficult basically three year process you know there's many people that a lot of physicians that actually commit suicide over stuff like this and I will tell you that thought crossed my mind especially early on it was very devastating to me for a devastating to my family I lost my home kids lost I had to take them out of school at Derry and it was very disruptive I lost time with my children lost all sorts of basically income you know I was able to make small income doing what I'm doing now but that was a very challenging situation for me and if it wasn't for very supportive girlfriend…"

**[他/她说过的]** [20190606001.md 00:00:06 – 00:01:39] "okay why are vegan doctors so weak all right before I getting into that I just wanted to have an update here so behind me is a New Mexico medical license it was just issued to me as of June 3rd 2019 this sort of ends a couple long year issue that I went through basically just to recap in 20 in this October 2017 I surrendered my New Mexico medical license to avoid a prolonged lengthy expensive legal battle with a hospital which I had a disagreement with the New Mexico Medical Board gave me an opportunity to be independently evaluated that was carried out in December of 2017 the results of that independent evaluation were made available in April of 2018 in May of 2018 in New Mexico Medical Board process those results and said you may reapply for your license at this time I have reapplied at that time it took them about six eight months to process that and then once the license application was completed the New Mexico Medical Board voted in February of 2019 to restore my license now that took another four months or so for that process to finally go through and that in May I was told that my license was going to be issued… it's available as of June 3rd this year so I'm fully licensed to practice medicine in New Mexico probably you know I used to have licenses in Texas and Arizona…"

**注**：原始决策点 (为何与医院冲突、为何拒绝继续做手术) 表达为 "I no longer wanted to put surgeries on people when rather they could benefit from lifestyle"，未明确点名"医院因我推 carnivore 处方而冲突"的因果，留白。

### 他/她说过的 vs 我推断的 vs 未发现

**他/她说过的 (有字幕证据)**：
- 医疗执照弃/恢复时间线 (2017-10 → 2019-06-03)
- 骨科/创伤外科 → 战时服役 → 拒做手术 → 倡导 lifestyle medicine 的职业转变
- 2006 年被派往 Bagram 6 个月 (40 岁生日)
- 《The Carnivore Diet》书在 2019-11-24 视频前"几天"出版
- 运营 Meat Heals / meatrx.com (carnivore 教练 + 研究图书馆)
- 公开个人化验 (testosterone、CACS、CRP 等)
- 运动决策：划船世界纪录尝试、举重、basketball

**我推断的 (从分散的"个人化"语句拼出叙事，但本人未明确决策性表达)**：
- 他写书 / 创 Meat Heals 的"破釜沉舟"动力，部分源于执照事件后"无法再临床执业"的现实
- "individual consultation"产品化是他在 2018-2019 主动建立"非临床医师"收入流的尝试

**未发现 (本轮 100 文件未命中)**：
- 出生年份与原籍 (他 2019-11-24 自报 "I'll turn 53 this year" → 2020 年他 53 岁，反推 1967 出生，但本轮未命中该数据；20191124001 是 2019 发布 → 53 岁 → 出生于 1966-1967，需后续文件核验)
- 何时首次吃 carnivore (2017 还是更早？)
- 结婚/离婚记录
- 为何从 keto 转 carnivore 的具体健康触发事件 (20180801001 仅说"在 keto 时我一直想…")
- Meat Heals / meatrx.com 创办日期与法律结构
- 早期决策 (医学训练、为何选 orthopedic) 的内部动机

- 含 "I went to medical school / orthopedic / trauma surgeon / graduated" 决策表述的文件: ~5 (20180721001, 20190222001, 20190606001, 20191124001, 20190711001)

## 轮 2/17 (2020-01-27 → 2022-11-29)

### 调研元数据

- 素材范围: `@ShawnBakerMD/20200127001.md` – `@ShawnBakerMD/20221129001.md` (100 个 .md 文件)
- 时间跨度: 2020-01-27 → 2022-11-29 (2 年 10 个月)
- 来源: YouTube auto-caption 直链下载 (与 R01 一致)

### 关键 grep 命令

| 命令 | 命中数 / 说明 |
|------|--------------|
| `grep -iE "I did\|I decided\|I started\|I wrote\|my book\|I quit\|I changed\|I stopped\|I was\|became\|career\|orthopedic\|surgery\|revero\|clinic\|company\|MeatRx\|launched"` | 命中文件多但"决策"语义稀薄（多数为"I did keto"等一般过去时）；高信号决策句集中在 20220827001, 20220503001, 20220311002, 20221113001, 20220613002, 20221129001 |
| `grep -iE "personal\|family\|wife\|kid\|life\|story\|journey"` | 大量命中但 99% 是营养/健康类"journey"，非家庭决策；高信号仅 20220311002（前 vegan 5 年 → 改吃肉，自称"afraid to tell you guys"）|
| `grep -iE "rowing\|world record\|athletic\|row\|run\|ran\|race\|racing\|marathon\|triathlon\|kilimanjaro\|basketball\|jiu jitsu\|BJJ"` | 20200503001 (rowing 20k m + 405 lb squat), 20220228001 + 20220728001 + 20221012001 (jiu jitsu 新增) |
| `grep -iE "MeatRx\|meterX\|metrx\|revero\|harvard\|clinical trial\|fundraiser"` | MeatRx 平台化、Harvard 研究募资为主轴；20200801001 ($72-73k/$200k) → 20200827001 ($117k) 增长 |
| `grep -iE "fifty\|51\|52\|53\|54\|55\|1966\|1967\|I'm a\|my age"` | 20220809001 提到"who just died at 55 my age"（暗示 2022 年 55 岁，反推 ~1967 出生）|

### 关键发现 (按"决策量级"排序)

#### 1. 启动并推进 Harvard-style 临床试验募资（2020-08: $72-117k/$200k → 2022 持续推进） —— **R02 新决策**

**[他/她说过的]** [20200801001.md 00:00:50 – 00:01:08] "update on the fundraiser 72 maybe 73 000 so far of the 200 000 needed... we are talking to professional researchers we're looking at uh you know uh basically uh clinical resource organizations... i will not be the primary researcher i'm not a researcher so but we are going to invest that money very wisely"

**[他/她说过的]** [20200827001.md 00:01:14 – 00:01:24] "the other thing is uh clinical trial the intervention trial that we're funding we are now nearly 117 000 raised so we are making good progress still need more support to get there... you talked with david ludwig eric westman several others lots of contract research organizations that are professionals that will be doing the study i won't be doing the study i'm not a researcher in any way... we're many once many months away from even getting it fully funded and then getting it finally designed and then implemented"

**未发现**：募资最终是否到 $200k、研究是否完成、研究结果是否发表；2022 文件中此话题淡出。

#### 2. 启动 "MeatRx" 平台化扩张 —— 2020-05 引入 fasting community、与 20+ 教练 + 多名医生协同运营

**[他/她说过的]** [20200503001.md 00:08:54 – 00:09:11] "on meteor XCOM we're introducing a fat fasting community some people use fasting it can be you know with or without a carnivore not many people combine it and it works quite well for those people... we're gonna like I said we're going to be expanding our repertoire to several items is beyond just mean only but you know obviously animal nutrition is an important part of it"

**[他/她说过的]** [20200503001.md 00:09:40 – 00:09:50] "welcome Mike muscle to meet our X our daily meeting for both the fasting and they in in the carnivore communities communities on that platform so if you haven't joined you can join for free sign up for a month you know pop into meeting ask questions directly"

**[他/她说过的]** [20200827001.md 00:02:06 – 00:02:09] "i talked about this a little bit on our meat rx podcast today but..."（从单一"meatheals.com 咨询" → "MeatRx podcast" 内容化转变）

**注**：与 R01 的"meatheals.com 个人咨询"产品相比，R02 出现 (a) 平台化社群运营 (b) podcast 内容输出 (c) 引入 fasting 等"动物营养之外的扩展" (d) 与外部医生/教练联合作业。从"个人顾问 → 平台 + 媒体"的产品化升级。

#### 3. 自我饮食策略切换：lean carnivore (2019-2020) → high-fat (85-90% 热量) carnivore (2020-08) → OMAD + leaner + refeed (2020-05)

**[他/她说过的]** [20200801001.md 00:00:19 – 00:00:34] "still doing a very high fat approach as you guys may or may not know when i was on a very lean carnivore diet now i'm on a very high fat 85 and 90 of my calories come from fat based diet so continuing to to do well with that"

**[他/她说过的]** [20200503001.md 00:05:03 – 00:05:15] "i usually when i'm on these days i'm eating leaner i'm he's eating one meal a day for the most part and then you don't have the refeed day where I eat several times any lot more fat but I'm so making it leaner"

**注**：饮食微调决策非主流媒体关注点，但是 R02 唯一明确的"我改了我的饮食方法"决策；可能与当时他"备赛 / 训练"目标相关。

#### 4. 持续公开宣示"愿接受药检 + 反 carnivore 圈内的 supplements 商业化"立场（2020-05 / 2022-11 两次重申）—— R01 决策的延续与升级

**[他/她说过的]** [20200503001.md 00:07:41 – 00:07:51] "you want to pay go ahead and pay have me tested I'll take the test I don't I don't mind it all but training my whole life taking many many drug tests over the years powerlifting USA powerlifting always passed"

**[他/她说过的]** [20221129001.md 00:02:32 – 00:03:13] "I've offered this many many times somebody wants to pay for testing I'm happy to do that I'm happy to pass any drug test or take any drug tests I've had Dr Garth Davis say he's going to do it then back then I had this guy uh durian writers idiot vegan you know said he's going to do it and he backed out... I'm happy to take the test I've passed many blood drug tests in the past uh when I was competing in powerlifting I've been a lifetime drug fee athlete I've been very proud of that fact that I've never taken steroids I don't take trt I'll take growth hormone"

**[他/她说过的]** [20221129001.md 00:00:40 – 00:01:10] "I've said this over and over again you know organ meat supplements and things like that overpriced um you know and and this guy liver king or Brian Johnson has been the face of one of them there's others very similar that that basically push the same thing and make a lot of money off this and I think this is very unfortunate because what would otherwise be a I think generally good messaging stop eating junk food get out in the sun exercise workout eat Whole Foods is unfortunately tainted by this stuff"

**注**：从"个人抗辩"到"行业内部批评 Liver King 等同行卖 supplements"——R02 出现了"gatekeeper 立场"转变：他开始把 carnivore 圈内"卖补充剂的人"和"无补充剂纯肉食者"区分开。

#### 5. 训练/比赛决策：rowing 20,000 m (2020-05)、squat 405 lb (2020-05)、jiu jitsu 加入 (2022 起)

**[他/她说过的]** [20200503001.md 00:10:01 – 00:10:07] "I've got to do 20,000 meters rowing this morning and then I'll be probably pulps and push-ups this afternoon most likely"

**[他/她说过的]** [20200503001.md 00:03:50 – 00:04:11] "this is after that I did some rowing and you'll see the form technique was a very different than what I do when I do longest throw this is just power production full big lay back pulling as hard as I can so I pull just a twenty second twenty second pull 30 strokes per minute... 405 which is 19 you know it strikes down a little bit normally I hit 20 pretty easily"

**[他/她说过的]** [20220228001.md 00:01:27 – 00:01:32] "sirloin steak and then i'll head out to jiu jitsu for you know some pretty intense jujitsu again all on just purely [water]"

**[他/她说过的]** [20220728001.md 00:02:38 – 00:02:41] "it puts everything in perspective when i'm at jiu jitsu and someone is trying to strangle me i.e"

**[他/她说过的]** [20221012001.md 00:04:52 – 00:04:57] "I gotta go Gotta go to jiu jitsu here in a few minutes so take care everybody bye-bye"

**注**：R01 主线 = rowing + powerlifting + world record 追求。R02 = rowing 减量、jiu jitsu 新增（出现于 2022-02 起，2022-07 / 2022-10 多次提及）。可推断他 2022 年对训练项目做了调整——由"重复冲击心肺/力量"转向"技术 + 实战对抗"。

#### 6. 个人体型对比叙事：2014 keto 280 lb / 240 lb → 2020 carnivore lean muscular —— 用作 carnivore 优于 keto 的"自证"

**[他/她说过的]** [20200503001.md 00:00:09 – 00:01:49] "this is spill ask me this is a picture of me back in 2014 when I was on a ketogenic diet you can see so big guy I can guarantee how some training every bit as hard as I'm training today... in that picture I was kind of fatter with my girl who's still my girl you know we've been together for now seven and a half years or so and she's she same as me and she looks great she was 47 in that picture as well... I had lost at this point I was 240 before that pork eat on standard American I was 280 so this is the difference in the progression here... the more on ze its say drugs right"

**[他/她说过的]** [20200503001.md 00:03:27 – 00:03:32] "obviously a dramatic difference of CM leaner more muscular probably about the same strength more or less of relaxed he may be slightly weaker currently just cuz I'm a little bit lighter"

**注**：体重数据 (280 → 240 → current lean) 是 R01 未披露的具体数字；自报 2014 keto 时 47 岁的女朋友仍是现女友 → 关系稳定。

#### 7. 反 vegan / 反 fake meat 立场延续与升级

**[他/她说过的]** [20220525001.md 00:01:23 – 00:01:28] "match for beyond meat this company is clearly struggling its ceo ethan brown"（Beyond Meat CEO 公开评价，作为反方证据）

**[他/她说过的]** [20221113001.md 00:03:00 – 00:03:11] "they would see tremendous gains in their health I put that in my book intentionally I knew that would sort of annoy some people because there's some people out there they're kind of food snots well if it's not there's high end grass finish this and that it's not going to get that's total guys plain old burger patties from McDonald's work just fine I've seen I've seen dozens and dozens if not hundreds of people reversed as easy just by doing that"

**[他/她说过的]** [20220918001.md 00:01:08 – 00:01:10] "there's been a couple people over the years that said hey I had I started a..."（未读完上下文，未发现完整决策）

**注**：R02 中他用"McDonald's plain patty"作为入门级 carnivore 入门路径，反"grass-fed 必胜"原教旨主义。

#### 8. R02 唯一的非本人决策陈述 —— 出现于 MeatRx 用户"我做了 30 天 carnivore 没效果"问答

**[他/她说过的]** [20220503001.md 00:00:02 – 00:00:40]（用户来信）"i have been doing the carnivore for over 30 plus days i wanted to experience the sleep benefits better clarity more energy etc i have not experienced any of this and i've had the worst time going to the loo i just don't get it what exactly am i missing here i did lose some weight but i didn't do it for that because i was already pretty fit already i desperately wanted to sleep i display one of the sleep benefits extra clear mind and no aches and pains and who wants to be constipated so dr baker any solutions"

**[他/她说过的]** [20220503001.md 00:00:55 – 00:01:14]（Baker 回应）"30 days may not be enough time to transition most people require somewhere between three to eight weeks generally you know again that's that's plus or take some people quicker some people longer"

**注**：这条本身不构成 Baker 的"决策"，但是他 2022 年的"客户咨询 / 决策框架"的具体示例——他用"过渡期不够长"作为默认诊断；该 user 的具体姓名 / 后续未在字幕中出现。

### 跨 R01-R02 决策变化总结

| 维度 | R01 (2018-2020-01) | R02 (2020-01-2022-11) |
|------|-------------------|----------------------|
| 商业形态 | meatheals.com 个人咨询 + 1对1 收费 + 教练认证 | MeatRx 平台 + Podcast + 多 community（fasting/carnivore）+ Harvard 研究募资 |
| 自我角色 | "ex-doctor / non-practicing"（被吊销执照后自我定位）| "lifetime drug free athlete" + "company"（公司化经营 + 健身人设强化）|
| 饮食方法 | keto → carnivore 转型 (具体日期缺) | 在 carnivore 内部从 lean → high-fat → OMAD + refeed 微调 |
| 训练 | powerlifting / rowing / world record 追求 | rowing 维持 + 405 lb squat + jiu jitsu 新增 (2022) |
| 反方 | 反 vegan / 反 seed oil 为主 | + 反 fake meat 公司 / 反 supplements 商业化（gatekeeper 角色）|
| 媒体合作 | Joe Rogan 嘉宾 1 次 | Joe Rogan 8 月回归 carnivore（"i never felt better than january"）—— 持续对话 |

### 未发现 (R02 本轮 100 文件未命中)

- Revero 公司化具体时间点 / 注册信息 / 团队构成 (虽然 20220613002.md 提到"our company" 但未具名)
- 婚姻/家庭结构变化（"my girl 仍是现女友"为唯一明确信号）
- 是否重新临床执业 (R01 提到 NM 执照恢复；R02 未提是否接诊)
- 是否完成 Harvard 研究
- 为何 2022 突然从 rowing 转向 jiu jitsu 的具体触发事件

- 含 "I did / I decided / I started" 高信号决策句: 5 个核心文件 (20200503001, 20200801001, 20200827001, 20220311002, 20221129001)
- 含 MeatRx / 平台 / podcast 决策: 至少 6 个文件 (20200503001, 20200801001, 20200827001, 20200918001 等)

## 轮 3/17 (2022-11-30 → 2023-04-28)

### 调研元数据

- 素材范围: `@ShawnBakerMD/20221130001.md` – `@ShawnBakerMD/20230428001.md` (100 个 .md 文件)
- 来源: YouTube auto-caption 直链下载
- 调研方向: Revero 完整化 / 平台化决策 / 个人运动突破 / 与主张强相关行动 / R02 → R03 变化

### 关键 grep 命令

| 命令 | 命中文件数 |
|------|----------|
| `grep -iE "I did\|I decided\|I started\|I wrote\|my book\|I quit\|I changed\|I stopped\|I was\|I became\|career\|orthopedic\|surgery\|revero\|clinic\|company\|MeatRx\|launched"` | 25 / 100 |
| `grep -iE "personal\|family\|wife\|kid\|life\|story\|journey"` | 多数 (松散命中) |
| `grep -iE "rowing\|rower\|world record\|athletic\|row\|run\|ran\|race\|racing\|marathon\|triathlon\|basketball\|track\|meet\|weight\|jiu jitsu\|BJJ"` | 全部 100 (高信号运动词高密度) |
| `grep -iE "I quit\|I stopped\|stopped practicing\|orthopedic surgeon"` | 仅 20221222001 命中关键段 |
| `grep -iE "56 year old\|turn 56\|my age"` | 4 个文件 (20221204001, 20221222001, 20230105002, 20230408001) |
| `grep -iE "Revero\|MeatRx"` | Revero 命中 1 个核心文件 (20221206001)；MeatRx 本轮 0 命中 |
| `grep -iE "supplement\|launch\|new company\|new product"` | 20221130001 命中 (反 supplement, 反 Liver King) |

### 关键发现 (按"决策量级"排序)

#### 1. 公开宣布 Revero Health co-founder 身份 + 即将上线 (2022-12-06) — R03 最大决策信号

**[他/她说过的]** [20221206001.md 00:02:31 – 00:02:52] "the system which is broken the Health Care system is broken I'm proud to be part of Rivera health I am a co-founder I'm a you know significant part I have a significant interest in that in that in that vehicle and we're looking forward to launching soon having our physicians and Healthcare Providers taking care of people and going after the true root cause of disease and not doing this let's just throw pills at stuff because that's crap that's not working"

**注**：他拼写为 "Rivera health"（实际公司名在 R02 / 其他上下文拼为 Revero）。本段是 R03 内唯一明确 co-founder 身份 + "launching soon" + "physicians/Healthcare Providers" 雇佣意图的语句。

**R02 → R03 变化**: R02 仅一处"our company"模糊提及，R03 首次公开 co-founder 自称 + 上线时间窗口信号。

#### 2. 启动/参与 Nashville 干预研究 (autoimmune: RA / psoriasis / UC) (2022-12-06)

**[他/她说过的]** [20221206001.md 00:01:44 – 00:01:59] "I'm heading off to Nashville this in a couple days and flying down there to help assist with a study that is going to be occurring an Interventional study looking at things like rheumatoid arthritis and psoriasis and ulcer colitis and other things autoimmune diseases which we are seeing within this community over and over and over…"

**[他/她说过的]** [20221206001.md 00:02:10 – 00:02:25] "I'm happy to see that going into the rigor of science to study this to see if this is truly really happening or is it just coincidence which I don't think it is I think it's there's really something there so I'm proud to be able to assist in getting that science done"

**决策点**: 同意肉身参与 (作为 co-PI 或顾问未明说) 一项聚焦 carnivore → autoimmune 改善的临床干预研究。这是从"倡导者"向"研究合作者"的角色升级。R02 未见此线索。

#### 3. 56 岁生日 + 终身 Natty 立场 + deadlift 复仇战 (2022-12-04 / 2022-12-22 / 2023-01-05)

**[他/她说过的]** [20221204001.md 00:00:17 – 00:00:20] "in good shape for uh soon to be 55 56 year old guy January 10th by the way in case" — **生日锚点: 1 月 10 日，1967 年生 (本轮他 56 岁)**。

**[他/她说过的]** [20221222001.md 00:00:25 – 00:00:39] "soon to turn 56 and I've not had this sort of oh my God your joints are going to fall apart thing and the reality is you know as an orthopedic surgeon I replaced a hell of a lot of joints"

**[他/她说过的]** [20230105002.md 00:00:37 – 00:00:50] "approximately one year later as I just about to turn 56 years of age uh by the way still a lifetime Natty I I ended up doing it 26 repetitions so I one upstairs efforting on this one"

**deadlift 复仇事件链**:
- 1 年前: 他 vs 传奇健美 Stan Efferding，trap bar 415 lb 死停硬拉 (dead stop, no touch & go)。Baker 22 reps，Efferding 25 reps，Baker 输。
- 1 年后 (2023-01-05): Baker 26 reps (one-up)，"still a lifetime Natty"（终身自然）。
**[他/她说过的]** [20230105002.md 00:00:00 – 00:01:52] 完整段。

**R01/R02 延续**: 终身 Natty 立场在 R02 已多次出现 ("I've not taken any of that stuff")，deadlift 强度 (500 lb x 13 reps 在 20221222001 中提到) 是 R01 700-lb 死拉传奇的当代延续。**R03 新出现**: 具名对手 (Stan Efferding) + "复仇"叙事 + 公开量化 rep 比赛。

#### 4. World Carnivore Month 第六年 (2023-01) + 个人运动目标制定 (pull-up rings / rowing / deadlift / BJJ)

**[他/她说过的]** [20230102001.md 00:00:05 – 00:01:36] "all right recap of uh day one world Carnival month this is the six years been going on" — **World Carnivore Month 已连续办 6 年** (每年 1 月)。

**[他/她说过的]** [20230102001.md 00:01:19 – 00:01:36] "I do have some goals as far as athletic performance you know the pull-up Rings I'd like to I don't know I hit nine today I should be in maybe 12 by the end of the month maybe 12 13 something like that I think that's pretty reasonable some rowing goals some deadlifting goals some Jiu Jitsu goals so that is what I'm looking at at the month"

**R03 新决策**: 把 World Carnivore Month 期间的运动目标 (pull-up rings 9→12, rowing, deadlift, BJJ) 作为公开打卡指标。**这是 R02 未见的"饮食 + 运动双轨目标化"模式**。

**[他/她说过的]** [20230107001.md 00:00:14 – 00:00:50] "this is my seventh going into my seventh year now um I will often include eggs sometimes I have dairy particularly in the form of cheese… but for World Carnival month every year I kind of tighten things up and go back to the roots and I've go I've gone easily two plus years on nothing but red meat salt and water and I do very well with that"

**[他/她说过的]** [20230107001.md 00:01:34 – 00:01:50] (体重) "I started out at 258.6 pounds now my weight is fluctuate I've hopped on a scale many times I've seen as high as 270 this week uh with clothes on… I was 255.6 or down about three pounds which you know I kind of expect to see that I'll lose a little bit of weight this month probably another you know five seven times five to seven pounds"

**[他/她说过的]** [20230107001.md 00:04:10 – 00:04:24] "the heaviest I've been in oh at least 10 years and so normally for the last 10 years I've been walking around at between 240 and 245 give or take there was one period of time where I got really really skinny doing some experiments with very low calorie higher fat approach but I lost too much muscle didn't enjoy that at all"

**R03 体重基线信号**: 历史稳定区间 240-245 lb (近 10 年)，本轮进入 WCM 时为 258-264 lb (过去 10 年最高)。R02 未见如此具体的体重披露。

#### 5. 反 Liver King / 反 organ meat supplement 公共立场 (2022-11-30) — 自我定位"非卖补充剂 carnivore"阵营

**[他/她说过的]** [20221130001.md 00:00:48 – 00:01:23] "this liver King stuff I don't care the guy's taking steroids or not it doesn't doesn't really matter much to me I mean I don't think anybody cares one way or the other but the the supplements the organ meat supplements I think by and large quite honestly are scammy and I think one of the reasons is the price markup is ridiculous… if you don't like to eat them just take my my pill… you don't like to eat them probably for a reason they're unnecessary quite honestly"

**[他/她说过的]** [20221130001.md 00:01:49 – 00:02:31] "you gotta buy my my supplement that to me is predatory it's scammy and it's disgusting in my view and I hope none of you guys fall for that I see a lot of people online commenting well I took all these supplements so I'm not going to mention Brands but um and I didn't notice an improvement and what should I do should I keep spending my money no don't spend your continued spending your money on this stuff take the money you save from that and buy a damn decent stake"

**[他/她说过的]** [20221130001.md 00:01:15 – 00:01:20] (引用背书) "my experience experience that's shown in the Harvard University paper the experience I've collected on thousands of people doing it they are unnecessary for you to be healthy"

**R02 → R03 变化**: R02 未见如此激烈的反 supplement 公共表态。R03 明确切割"卖补充剂的 carnivore 网红"与"卖肉的 carnivore 倡导者" — 这是他商业定位的边界声明 (我不卖补充剂)。

#### 6. 反 seed oil / 反 vegan 行动延续 (2023-01-06 / 2023-01-10 / 2023-01-31)

**[他/她说过的]** [20230106001.md 00:01:42 – 00:02:54] (glyphosate 视频) — 把 glyphosate 累积问题直接挂钩"vegan/plant-based pea protein 推动" — 工具化"plant-based 推广"作为对 carnivore 的反向论据。

**[他/她说过的]** [20230131001.md 00:05:58 – 00:06:01] "my advice is just to avoid surgery oil in general whether it's whole grain or not" — (字幕识别错误) 实际是 "seed oil"。延续 R01/R02 一贯立场。

**[他/她说过的]** [20230110001.md] 反复出现 "kids" 词频高，对应"儿童 GLP-1 处方 / 13 岁起 gastric bypass 手术" 的反儿童代谢药表态。

**R03 决策量级变化**: 反对对象从"成人选择"扩展到"儿童/青少年" — 这是新论域。

#### 7. "Lift heavy 不毁关节"立场 (2022-12-22) — 与个人行为 (500 lb x 13 reps deadlift) 闭环

**[他/她说过的]** [20221222001.md 00:00:31 – 00:01:35] "as an orthopedic surgeon I replaced a hell of a lot of joints a lot of knee joints hip joints shoulder joints and I'll speak to you know what I saw and what the literature seems to support and I will tell you the short answer is lifting heavyweights does not destroy your joints… the majority of the people that consumed knee Replacements are females… it's because we know that metabolic disease is driving arthritis to a large part"

**[他/她说过的]** [20221222001.md 00:03:00 – 00:03:15] "when I was 26 I dead lifted 700 pounds for the first time I did that consistently until I was about 44 was the last time I did lift it that heavy"

**[他/她说过的]** [20221222001.md 00:03:23 – 00:03:36] "deadlifting 500 pounds like I did a couple days ago for you know 13 reps was you know not that heavy I mean if I can do it 13 times and obviously it's not that heavy right so it doesn't make sense so so don't fear getting stronger don't fear putting more weight on the bar"

**R01/R02 延续**: 700 lb 死拉在 R01 已记录 (20191124001)。R03 新增: 公开量化近作 (500 lb x 13 reps @ 56 岁) + "代谢病驱动关节炎，不是力学" 的反主流骨科共识框架。

#### 8. 反 GLP-1 / Ozempic 长期安全立场 (2023-01-04) — 引用 Peter (Hyperlipid)

**[他/她说过的]** [20230104001.md 00:00:29 – 00:04:50] 引用 Hyperlipid 博主对 GLP-1 agonist (semaglutide / liraglutide) 脂肪细胞 hyperplasia + 反弹饥饿 + 乳腺癌信号的系统性批评。

**[他/她说过的]** [20230104001.md 00:07:05 – 00:07:25] "it's not good to be the last guy but it's not good to be the first guy you know it's kind of like just kind of sit back and watch for a while and see what happens… give it give it a little bit of time maybe a couple years and see uh what's actually happening here because these drugs while you know they certainly are causing a lot of people lose weight there's no doubt about that is there going to be some downward effect Downstream Perhaps"

**R03 决策量级变化**: GLP-1 减重药 2022-2023 突然爆火，Baker 提前选边 — 这是他把"carnivore > 药物"主张延伸到"非手术 / 非 GLP-1 减重" 的新前沿。

**[他/她说过的]** [20230104001.md 00:07:46 – 00:07:55] "World Carnival month day four is underway I'm starting out a couple ribeye steaks uh things are going well uh I'm like I said I just feel wonderful doing this this way with just straight beef and beef beef and salt and water"

#### 9. 终身 Natty + 拒绝 TRT (2023-04-08) — 56 岁明确拒绝睾酮替代

**[他/她说过的]** [20230408001.md 00:04:55 – 00:05:00] "testosterone replacement you know I say that as a 56 year old male who doesn't do any of that stuff I have no desire to go on this stuff for a number of reasons"

**[他/她说过的]** [20230408001.md 00:05:30 – 00:05:45] "if you do these other things and you and you really really spend a year maybe two years focusing on a lifestyle you can perhaps avoid ever having to go on testosterone"

**[他/她说过的]** [20230105002.md 00:00:37 – 00:00:38] "still a lifetime Natty"

**R01/R02 延续**: 反对 TRT / 反对 steroid 在 R01 已有。本轮 56 岁明确表达"不打算用"。

#### 10. 训练 + 教年轻人 (2022-12-06) — 公共健身 / 私教形象建立

**[他/她说过的]** [20221206001.md 00:00:19 – 00:00:37] (Steak and Butter Gal 的 boyfriend Max 来 Baker 家，Baker 教他训练) "he teaches my son to play piano and I said well you know you do that I'll help you with some workouts and so I put him through and then we have somebody staying at our house from France that also is about both the young kids are both in their early 20s and it's fun for me to sort of teach them about how to to train"

**R03 新决策信号**: 家庭被反复用作训练场 (教儿子、教儿子钢琴老师、教住家法国访客)。这是"自办私人教练 + 客厅健身房"模式的人格化展示，与 World Carnivore Month 的运动目标呼应。

### 推断 (vs 他/她说过的)

**[我推断]**: Baker 体重基线从 240-245 升至 258-264 lb 可能与他 56 岁代谢变化 + 长期高强度 deadlift 训练 + WCM 前恢复 dairy/eggs 有关 — 但他在 20230107001 明说"there was one period of time where I got really really skinny doing some experiments with very low calorie higher fat approach but I lost too much muscle"，说明他对减重有"试错 → 拒绝"的历史。他倾向"接受体重"而非"主动减脂"。

**[我推断]**: Revero 即将 launch 的时间点 (2022-12) 恰逢 World Carnivore Month 启动 (2023-01) — 商业化推进 + 公共文化宣教同步，但他在 20230102001 / 20230107001 完全没提 Revero，可能有意识区分"产品发布"与"日常 carnivore 内容"。

### 关键发现 (按"决策量级"排序)

#### 1. **Revero 公开 launch 临近** (2023-06-20 旧金山出差 + 2023-07-28 嵌入教学) — R04 顶级新决策

**[他/她说过的]** [20230620001.md 00:00:02 – 00:00:08] "okay I was recently in San Francisco and I was there to help get ready to launch our company Rivera as you guys know Rivero his mission is to reverse chronic disease using nutrition lifestyle and so on and so forth we're actually treating the root cause it's not playing the symptom management game which no one ever gets healthy and everybody stays sick they stay medicated Pharmacy makes lots of money and they're happy but most of the patients are"

**[他/她说过的]** [20230620001.md 00:00:46 – 00:00:51] "saw you know a lot of the beautiful sights and worked on the company I found quite a bit of meat there in San Francisco I'm happy reports I went to one place which was recommending me called The House of Prime Rib and it was pretty darn good I got the double King Henry cut which is giant double thick serving of prime rib I think it was around 30 ounces or something like maybe 32 ounces"

**[他/她说过的]** [20230728001.md 00:05:16 – 00:05:20] (How To Get Started 视频嵌入段落) "the good news is as Rivera stands up we'll have health care physicians that are expertly trained on how to do that you know it's a good idea perhaps to taper off your fiber and vegetable fruit consumption gradually some people have a better transition with that remember our gut microbiome was going to change over time"

**[他/她说过的]** [20230728001.md 00:05:46 – 00:05:50] "the one I'm involved in is called carnivore.d diet and we have daily meetings on there every single day encouraging asking or asking and answering questions of people and it's very very helpful in fact one of the most important quote-unquote supplements is diet is a support system I think it's very very important"

**R03 → R04 决策变化**:
- R03 (2022-12) 提及 Revero 仍是模糊 "our company"
- R04 (2023-06-20) **首次公开承认"launch our company"**，出差到 SF 实地推进
- R04 (2023-07-28) 公开把 Revero 嵌入"如何开始 carnivore"教学流 ("as Rivera stands up we'll have health care physicians...") — 这是把 Revero 作为 carnivore 入门基础设施的明确定位
- 自称"Rivero his mission is to reverse chronic disease" — 明确产品使命陈述
- 关键表态: "treating the root cause" / "not playing the symptom management game" — 与主流医疗体系对立的定位
- 跨州出差 (Texas → SF)、跨州营销 (在 SF 找肉食场景) 暗示 Revero 总部 / 主要团队可能在 SF (R04 未明确团队地址)

**MeatRx 退场信号**: R04 100 文件中 0 命中 "MeatRx" — 与 R03 (0 命中) 保持一致。**[我推断]**: MeatRx 已完成历史使命 (作为 Meat Heals 2.0 平台), 现已被 Revero 取代; Baker 已从平台运营转向"实体诊所 + 远程医疗"模式。

#### 2. **国际化推进: 2023-05 计划访英见 Dr David Unwin** (2023-05-19 公开预告)

**[他/她说过的]** [20230519001.md 00:02:17 – 00:02:30] "Studies have shown that healthy individuals see no difference in kidney function whether they eat a high protein or a low protein diet if you already have kidney disease or extreme kidney damage even then you know there are studies out there one in particular Dr David Unwin who I'm going to visit next week in the UK has actually published data on this showing that people with compromised kidney function on higher protein diets low carb diets actually reverse that so protein does not damaging your kidneys"

**R03 → R04 决策变化**: R03 未见 Baker 国际行程。R04 首次公开预告跨大西洋行程 — 与英国 low carb / keto 医学界 (Unwin 是 UK low carb 旗手医生) 建立正式联系。**[我推断]**: 这次访问是 Revero 国际化 / 医学顾问网络搭建的早期信号, 后续 (R05+) 应验证是否产出合作内容 (论文 / 视频 / Revero 国际分部)。

#### 3. **KetoCon 2023 演讲** (2023-04-29 Austin TX) — 公共 carnivore 推广里程碑

**[他/她说过的]** [20230429002.md 00:03:08 – 00:03:43] (在 carnivore bar 摊位) "Robin Switzer rather did a wonderful job setting this up and organizing this takes about a year to put this on and I certainly believe a great number of guests were slave to speak I spoke on on the last day towards the end of the conference you know when I got there I immediately was greeted by four hours of about a four hour line but just saying hi to folks and I got to tell you I've met so so many incredible people always these these wonderful stories of just these Health victories you know overcoming these just tremendous illnesses things like multiple sclerosis Huntington's Korea rheumatoid arthritis from things like that to you know many people reversing diabetes coming off all their medications uh and typically people losing you know you know well in excess of 100 pounds I would couple that both of them had lost 200 pounds so it's an incredible uh incredible note to meet and see all the folks"

**[他/她说过的]** [20230429002.md 00:04:44 – 00:04:58] "ketocon has morphed into nearly a carnivore con because most it seems like most of the attendees were at least fully carnivore or mostly carnivore heading in that direction we've seen that what works is what works and more and more people are realized the value of including a lot of meat in the diet and I think it's a wonderful thing so don't forget my guys to eat meat and we'll talk to you soon"

**R03 → R04 决策变化**:
- R03 Baker 多次在 World Carnivore Month 主场活动; R04 KetoCon 出席确认 keto 主流会议已 "morphed into nearly a carnivore con"
- 自报告 4 小时长队 (R01/R02/R03 历轮未见如此具体的"线下热度"量化)
- 公共政策叙事升级: "more and more people are realized the value of including a lot of meat in the diet" — 这是 "carnivore = 文化潮流" 表述
- **[他/她说过的]** [20230429002.md 00:02:23 – 00:02:31] (在 carnivore bar 摊位做现场 mini-interview) 推出 Noble Origins (organ-based 蛋白棒 / 蛋白粉 / colostrum / beef protein isolate 等 carnivore 加工食品品牌) — Baker 站台但**未明确**该品牌是他的或独立第三方的, 语气是"被介绍给我的" (被 Phil Misi 引荐)

**第 4 小时长队量化** = Baker 公共影响的 "demand signal" — 这是 R01-R03 未见的 "线下活动排队时长" 直接数据点, 可用于衡量他品牌热度。

#### 5. **持续强化 "carnivore 入门指南" 内容生产** (2023-07-28 长视频 How To Get Started)

**[他/她说过的]** [20230728001.md 00:00:08 – 00:00:18] "more people are trying to do this remember I wrote the actual first book on the Carnival diet I in fact came up with that term the carnivore diet so I've got probably more experience than just about anyone on the planet with this particular diet and so I'd like to share my experience with you guys"

**[他/她说过的]** [20230728001.md 00:03:16 – 00:03:19] "you can survive indefinitely on just beef as long as it's got enough fat in there and that's that's the deal you got to have enough fat to run your machine energetically the protein and beef is outstanding and it is really a complete food"

**[他/她说过的]** [20230728001.md 00:04:11 – 00:04:17] "when you stop eating carbohydrates your body will start to diariese out sodium insulin is sending signals for the kidneys to hold on to sodium we don't have insulin being secreted in as much quantities and therefore our kidneys tend to let go more sodium and we lose a bit more water also the additional protein the higher protein content of this diet also tends to require a little bit more water"

**[他/她说过的]** [20230728001.md 00:05:01 – 00:05:14] "now if you're a diabetic or taking many other medications you might have to lower some of those medication uses you know diabetics often have to decrease insulin if they're on that or decrease any other medications now make sure you consult your health care professional when you're doing that and the good news is as Rivera stands up we'll have health care physicians that are expertly trained on how to do that"

**R04 新信号**: Baker 开始把 carnivore 入门教程显式对准"零基础新人":
- 30-90 天适应期承诺

- 电解质管理 (sodium + water)
- 糖尿病患者药物 taper 警告
- 推广 carnivore.diet 社区
- 自我定位: "I wrote the actual first book" / "I came up with that term" — **强化"carnivore 命名者"自我史**

#### 6. **反 supplement 立场持续 + 实证强化** (2023-04-29 carnivore bar 站台但拒绝背书)

**[他/她说过的]** [20230429002.md 00:01:33 – 00:01:50] (在 carnivore bar 摊位试用 Noble Origins 制品) "tell me about this what is it called mobile little Noble Origins yeah so we got a nose and tail Shake right here so we got beef protein isolate organ complex grass-fed collagen colostrum so all the good stuff chocolate vanilla I know you're an unflavored guy but maybe we'll get you on the chicken is that is that going to give me super powers if I take it"

**[我推断]**: Baker 在 Revero 即将 launch 的语境下, 选择"包容独立 carnivore 周边品牌"以维护 carnivore 社区凝聚力, 但个人品牌仍坚守"whole food"边界。

#### 9. **"我不是 anti-doctor, anti-medicine" 软化叙事** (2023-05-19 Your Doctor May Be A DUMMY 视频, 但实质是反 medical establishment 而非反个人医生)

**R04 新表态**: Baker 明确"我不反医生个人, 我反医学体系说蠢话" — 这是 R03 强对立姿态的微妙软化, 准备为 Revero 招募"开明医生"做铺垫 (Revero 模式 = 招募对 nutrition 友好的医生, 而非完全反医)。

**5 个具体反 medical establishment 立场**:
1. gastroenterologist 说"diet has nothing to do with IBS/IBD" — 反
2. 医生说"protein is bad for your kidneys" — 反 (引用 Dr Unwin 反证)
3. 医生说"nutrition has no role in mental health" — 反
4. 医生把 carnivore 视为 fad — 反
5. 医生无差别推荐 statin / metformin — 反 (R03 已有, R04 延续)

**R04 新增决策**: 把"5 signs"模板化, 暗示后续可能持续生产 "Your Doctor Said X" 系列内容, 建立"医学错误观念清单"。

#### 10. **保留 R03 决策**: 56 岁无 TRT + 终身 Natty + 反儿童 metabolic 药物 + 800 lb deadlift 目标 + 7 年 carnivore 自我定位

**[他/她说过的]** [20230429001.md 00:01:38 – 00:01:46] "I've been on now carnivore for around seven years and I and I definitely think the diet has something to do with that you know and I've always trained hard my entire life there's never been a period since I've been about 12 or 13 years of age that I haven't trained hard"

**[他/她说过的]** [20230429001.md 00:00:25 – 00:00:28] "I'm almost well I'm 56 and almost 56 and a half"

**[他/她说过的]** [20230429002.md 00:01:56 – 00:01:59] (对儿子) "I still haven't finished puberty yet all right but this man's the reason why I went carnivore right here so he's the best all right guys awesome" — **R04 关键父子信号**: 儿子出现在 KetoCon carnivore bar 摊位, 仍与父亲一起

**[他/她说过的]** [20230501001.md 00:00:10 – 00:00:14] "I did this uh three rounds my son is kind enough to do the filming out here and so I think he did a pretty good job" — **儿子 = 摄影助手** 出现, 持续 R03 "教儿子" 模式

**[他/她说过的]** [20230611002.md 00:02:57 – 00:03:01] (引用某女性嘉宾) "she no longer could continue There were some months where I didn't have my period I went to get checked" — 引用 carnivore 治愈 amenorrhea 故事, 与 R03 反儿童代谢药呼应

**[他/她说过的]** [20230611002.md 00:04:06 – 00:04:15] (引用某女性嘉宾) "until my second year of incorporating animal protein that I started eating meat consistently that's when I started to eat more beef because of the B12 and..." — Baker 反复邀请女性嘉宾 (本轮 20230611002 整集是 carnivore 治愈女性 amenorrhea + 抑郁 故事)

### 推断 (vs 他/她说过的)

**[我推断]**: Revero 在 2023-06 处于"预 launch"状态 (旧金山出差 + 团队推进), 2023-07-28 视频 (2023-07-28) 把 Revero 嵌入教学, 暗示 2023-Q3 (R05 范围) 应有正式 launch 事件。R05 应验证: 是否有 Revero 上线公告 / 第一个接诊案例 / 媒体曝光。

**[我推断]**: "Texas 迁居"暗示 Baker 离开 New Mexico (R01-R03 长期基地) 进入 Texas (Houston / Austin)。这可能与 Revero 选址或 Texas 的 no-state-income-tax / 牛肉生产州身份有关。

**[我推断]**: Baker 的 "Carnivore 入门" 长视频 (2023-07-28) 出现在 Revero 预 launch 后 1 个月, 暗示他正在建立"carnivore 知识梯度" — 基础入门视频 (2023-07-28) → Revero 临床干预 (R05+) 的导流管道。

**[我推断]**: 56.5 岁年龄锚定 (2023-04-29) + Revero launch (2023-06-20) + "I wrote the first book" 自我史 (2023-07-28) — 这是 Baker 品牌"权威化" 阶段 (从 carnivore 倡导者 → carnivore 教父 + 临床机构创始人), 跨越 R03-R04 持续强化。

### 关键发现(他/她公开说过的)

**1. 医疗执照事件 — 首次完整版自述** (2023-08-16, `20230816002.md`)
回应 Dr. Muhammad Alo (Toledo Ohio 心血管 DO) 的攻击视频,Baker **第一次**详细讲述执照事件时间线:
- 2016 年前后与某医院发生 "long battle",医院对他提出指控
- 医疗委员会给他两条路:"defend at the medical board level" (2 年 + 25 万美元律师费) **OR** "temporarily surrender"(自愿放弃)→ 独立评估
- 他选了后者,因 "not wanting to wait several years" + "couldn't afford" 25 万美元
- 独立评估结果: "nothing wrong with what you do practice wise"
- 执照归还,此后已 **续证 3 次**,目前 "active again until 2026"
- 关键自我辩护:"there's not even a single incidence of a malpractice record"
- 长期叙事定调:把这件事归因于 "I started talking about uh less fix[ing] disease with nutrition" 后与医院发生冲突
- **R05 新信号**: 这是 Baker 首次在视频中给出如此完整的执照时间线+续证状态自述。R01-R04 较少见他主动公开续证时间

**2. 离婚 / 见不到孩子** (2023-08-30, `20230830001.md` "Don't let it define you")
- 主动提及个人史: "the other one I threw my divorce and wasn't able to see my kids on a regular basis"
- 当时应对方式: "I started jumping around just just to kind of mindlessly numb myself in a way that was... ultimately somewhat protective"
- 自我框架: "tempered steel you just become stronger due to it" + "go to the gym and just... become a better person"
- **R05 新信号**: 在 R04 中很少见他在公开视频里这么直接说 "throw my divorce" / "wasn't able to see my kids on a regular basis"。这是个人化叙事的明显升级

**3. 父亲形象 / 童年故事** (2023-09-12, `20230912001.md` "A challenge for men!!")
- 首次明确回忆童年: "I was about nine eight nine maybe ten ten years old... wrestling around" 然后 "punched him right in the nose" → 鼻子流血
- "you literally look up to your father... many of us unfortunately don't have that situation"
- 把这段记忆联系到 "food addiction" / "obesity as bad father example"
- **R05 新信号**: 父亲维度首次在公开视频出现具体童年细节

**4. 年龄 / 体型自报** (2023-08-16, `20230816002.md`)
- "I'm six foot five I'm 250 I'm 56 years of age"
- "I'm working out for 40 uh 44 almost 44 years now"
- 强调 "drug-free competitions my entire career" / 主动说愿意接受 drug test
- **R05 数据点**: 1966/1967 年生 (2023-56),自称训练 ~44 年 (从 12-13 岁起)
- 间接:在希腊有 "neck injury" 限制训练 (20230830001),正在康复中

**5. 儿子在 R05 出现** (2023-08-03, `20230803002.md` "Is this the best BBQ in the world??")
- 2023-08-03 带着 "the kids" 在 Lockhart, Texas (BBQ 圣地) + San Antonio 看 Alamo + Austin
- 关键句: "and then off to Austin Texas where he was born he's not been back since he's been born"
- → 儿子出生在 Austin,Texas,**儿子从未回过出生地** (Baker 当时在 Texas 行医的进一步佐证)
- **R05 新信号**: 地理证据:儿子在 Austin 出生,Baker 在 Texas 期间行医。与 R04 中他已经迁离 Texas 的判断吻合

**6. Reverso/Revero / MeatRx 完全缺位**
- 0 命中 "Revero" + 0 命中 "MeatRx" 在 R05 100 文件中
- → R05 期间,他**没有**在任何视频里讨论公司业务 (Revero 后续、MeatRx 状态)
- 全部精力放在:反 vegan 故事、fiber 科学、LDL 论证、批评者回应、动物权益反击

**7. 7 年公开反主流信号 (自报)** (2023-08-16, `20230816002.md`)
- "I've been criticized non-stop for the last seven years since I started talking about uh less fixed disease with nutrition"

**8. 政治议题主动介入** (2023-09-10, `20230910001.md` "Yes, they are, in fact, insane!!")

- 在希腊期间:有 neck injury、每天 "2 300 push-ups... as my main form of exercise"

### 关键发现 (按"决策量级"排序)

#### 2. 颈部严重伤病 — 7 周前 Jiu-Jitsu 受伤 → 启动"医疗 + 饮食 + 训练"组合治疗 (2023-09-30 起多次更新)

**[他/她说过的]** [20230930001.md 00:00:37.71 – 00:00:52.55] "7 weeks and two days ago now I injured my neck while doing Jiu-Jitsu and uh I ended up with uh what is likely a large herniated disc between the C6 and C1 C7 level in my spine which causes something called C7 radiculopathy which is basically means the nerve root is being compressed and it's causing quite a few symptoms uh for me that means uh you know my index and my middle finger are completely numb… pain that that varies sometimes as low as a three sometimes as high as an eight"

**[他/她说过的]** [20230930001.md 00:02:39.71 – 00:03:05.78] "in an effort to avoid hopefully needing surgery for this because a surgery for this depending on the exact size and location of the disc rupture uh may may may result in either a disc replacement or Fusion at the neck or perhaps a decompression from the from the back either way options I'm not excited about certainly 'd rather uh get through this without surgery… my dietary shift is going to be away from consuming um as much protein and probably more more fat-based"

**[他/她说过的]** [20231001001.md 00:01:06.39 – 00:01:25.51] "generally I I really never really produce a whole lot of ketones you know some of it I'm probably pretty good at utilizing it uh and so this is a therapeutic attempt by me to again there's pretty good literature showing that uh a state of ketosis seems to help with neuroinflammation which is you know what I'm currently dealing with… I'm reducing the overall amount of food I'm eating uh my caloric intake uh you know on on uh so today is Sunday so on Friday with 1,600 calories yesterday it was probably around I 23 2400 calories that's as opposed to normally 3,000 to 4,000 calories"

**[他/她说过的]** [20231001001.md 00:02:42.14 – 00:02:47.03] "my morning I measured my blood ketones and they are at 2.6 millimolar which is the highest I've ever seen in my life quite honestly I've never really seen much more outside of the one point something so that's that's you know certainly uh indicative that I am you know more deeply in ketosis than perhaps I've ever measured"

**[他/她说过的]** [20231001001.md 00:03:43.48 – 00:03:46.87] "as you guys know I'm an orthopedic surgeon I used to operate on people's necks I'm very familiar with this condition and the the efficacies of various treatments that are out there um and so we'll to see how it goes"

**[他/她说过的]** [20231010003.md 00:00:31.27 – 00:00:52.67] "some relief although I've been doing it less consistently because I I didn't feel like I Pro I got consistent improvement with that I've been going to physical therapy which consists of basically some positioning uh stretching some nerve uh gliding activities… the final thing that I have been doing is this higher fat uh ketogenic style carnivore diet um… I just had an MRI yesterday I I've got the disc for that so I need to I need to look at those and and see what the U you know what what what it shows"

**[他/她说过的]** [20231012002.md 00:01:18.43 – 00:01:57.74] "you guys know I've been doing a ketogenic style of carnivore diet you know I'm not I'm not doing a keto diet where I'm eating you know uh you know blueberries and salads and stuff like this is pure meat it's just meat and fat so I've been up upping my fat ratios with fattier cuts of meat I use some butter from time to time and you know just that's been about I think a little over two weeks give or take something like that and I have to say honestly uh symtom wise pain reduction is down significantly um I've been able to sleep again which as you guys might imagine is a very good thing… still I've got some pretty good numbness in the fingers but the pain is kind of centralized it means instead of going down my arm it's kind of going back up here which is you know more more you know closer to to the source which is generally considered a good uh a good sign so anyway you know fingers crossed thanks for all the hopes and prayers and suggestions guys"

**[他/她说过的]** [20231010001.md 00:01:08.32 – 00:01:16.55] "I mentioned I'm doing lots of things in acupuncture yesterday I I did some you know training this is a a little leg workout I did yesterday so I'm still able to train you know albeit I got to"

**[我推断]** 2023-09 中下旬某次 Jiu-Jitsu (柔术) 训练中颈部受伤, 致 C6-C7 椎间盘突出 + C7 神经根病 + 手指麻木; Baker 的决策路径 = **多模态并行** (颈椎牵引 + 物理治疗 + 神经滑动 + 针灸 + 减重 + 升脂降蛋白的生酮版 carnivore + 每周 MRI 监测); 决策核心目的 = 避免手术 (他本人熟悉自己当年做的颈椎手术类型, 明确说"either a disc replacement or Fusion at the neck")

**[他/她说过的]** [20231002001.md 00:04:07.14 – 00:04:16.61] "still still kind of not doing well ketones are high though I've got my Ketone levels going up there I think I'd hit 6.1 yesterday which is the highest I've ever hit I woke up again this morning at 2.6 um but you know like I said early days"

**[他/她说过的]** [20231020004.md 00:00:08.63 – 00:00:16.43] "lbs on the Kabuki strength trap bar just doing sets of 10 I think I did three sets of this again still fairly light it's not that much of a circle but I'm like I said taking it easy I did some regular squats with the belt Squat and"

**[他/她说过的]** [20231013003.md 00:00:01.59 – 00:00:24.71] "all right a little workout actually this the first time I've been deadlifting since I kind of had a neck injury so I just came went back with a relatively lightweight this is uh 325 lbs on the Kabuki strength trap bar here two 55s and a 25 the bar weighs 55 lbs so I did it for uh 10 repetitions times five rounds for a total of 50 reps every 90 seconds"

**[我推断]** 受伤并未让 Baker 停止训练, 反而成为他公开每周分享"受伤后仍训练"的内容主线; **R05 没有这个伤病**, R06 颈部伤病是新增重大健康事件, 直接影响了他的饮食策略 (从标准 carnivore 转到 keto-carnivore 升脂版本)

#### 5. Revero 临上线前的舆论铺垫 — 案例营销 (Lyme 病 Cassie / 4 期结肠癌 Jeff / 心血管 Michael Riley)

**[他/她说过的]** [20231013001.md 00:01:11.84 – 00:02:00.30] "he was diagnosed with stage four terminal colorl cancer now he'd been on various you know kind of a standard American diet vegetarian diet at some point prior to this and with that diagnosis he was told he had metastasis to his liver uh there was nothing that could be done with that surgically… he went on as you guys may may expect a carnivore diet and so now we are now 18 months after the diagnosis so he's now doubled the average life expectancy he's had multiple uh scans four or five uh CAT scans to look at the progression of the tumor and in every single one of them it's continued to shrink and shrink and Shrink so much so that the the oncologist says he almost never sees this happen… my son's spending time became quite emotional about that"

**[他/她说过的]** [20231012002.md 00:00:02.28 – 00:00:38.11] "I had to respond to this um and it was about a guy named Michael Riley who had had ended up having a triple bypass after going carnivore… Terry symptom saying oh oh carnivore caused this and not and carnivore people are silly and don't know what they're talking about and you should do a Mediterranean di well it turns out that the guy who this was video was about guy Michael Riley um actually watched video that I did and here's what he had to say so basically he said yeah pretty much what you said was spot on"

### R05 vs R06 决策变化

**R05 延续** (语义和叙事姿态保持一致):
- 反 vegan / 反 GLP-1 / 反 body positivity 姿态
- "pharma captured healthcare" 叙事
- "I am an orthopedic surgeon" 自我身份锚点
- 250K 订阅里程碑 (R06 跨过)
- 反复给临床建议 (weight loss / 慢性病 / metabolic syndrome)
- 客户证言营销模式 (MeatRx 模式 → Revero 模式)
- 每周多次"健康新闻 + 自我训练 + 评论"组合的视频结构

**R05 → R06 重大变化 / 新出现**:

| 维度 | R05 状态 | R06 状态 | 变化意义 |
|------|---------|---------|---------|
| **Revero** | 完全消失 (0 命中) | 19 命中, "days away from launch" | 核心事业重新进入主线 |
| **Revero 定位** | "purely carnivore company" (R04 暗示) | "not a purely carnivore company but will certainly be using it when indicated" | 临床定位松动, 向综合代谢病平台演化 |
| **等待名单** | 未提及 | 1,000+ → 几 thousand | 用户增长超预期 |
| **重大个人披露** | "throw my divorce" / "couldn't see my kids" | **"两次婚姻 + 两次非洲蜜月 + 两次离婚"** (具体量化) | 离婚叙事深化 |
| **个人健康事件** | R05 健康, 高强度训练 | **颈部 C6-C7 椎间盘突出 + C7 神经根病** (Jiu-Jitsu 致伤) | 重大健康危机 |
| **饮食策略** | 标准 carnivore | **"ketogenic style of carnivore"** (升脂 80-85%, 降蛋白, 减热量 1500-2400 vs 正常 3000-4000) | 因伤病被迫调整 |
| **运动强度** | 高强度常规训练 | 受伤后从 deadlift / sprint 回退到 trap bar / 短距 sprint | 主动降低 + 边练边治 |
| **儿子 / 家庭符号** | 偶尔出现 | **完全从第一人称叙述消失** (0 命中) | 家庭维度收缩 |
| **军旅 / PTSD 创伤** | 0 命中 | **"WAR IS HELL" 两期 + 自我暴力倾向披露** | 心理创伤维度首次明确 |
| **国际出差** | 几乎无 | 德国讲座 / 奥兰多会议 / 引用挪威调查 | 国际化加速 |
| **案例营销** | MeatRx 老用户为主 | 癌症 / Lyme / 心血管 三个高戏剧性案例 | Revero 上线前的高强度舆论铺垫 |
| **临床研究 / 数据** | "battle tactics" 动员 | 引用 BMJ 大样本 (136,137 人) + 挪威 18,000 人调查 | 科学化论证升级 |
| **政治化** | Oregon IP13 动员 + Trump 暗讽 | **"WAR IS HELL" 直接政治评论** | 政治化从动员升级为长篇内容 |
| **反 body positivity** | 已存在 | **"telling the lies that we've been sold about obesity especially childhood obesity are not true"** + "I'm willing to state my medical career on this" | 强度升级 + 把医学生涯作为赌注 |
| **书籍** | R05 完全无 | 完全无 | 持续沉默 |
| **MeatRx** | R05 完全无 | 完全无 | 品牌可能已被 Revero 取代 |

**未发现 (在 R06 100 文件中)**:
- Baker 现任妻子 / 女友 / 伴侣的任何信息 (R01 提过 girlfriend, R05 没提, R06 0 命中)
- Baker 儿子 / 女儿 / 父亲 / 母亲作为第一人称叙述
- Baker 居住地变更 (R05 在 Europe/Greece, R06 没明说但出差欧洲)
- 新公司、新合作伙伴 (除 Revero 外)
- 新出版协议 / 新书 / 新研究合作 (除自己诊所外)
- 任何关于 R01 提到的医疗诉讼 / 州医委会事件后续
- 任何关于"goFundMe" 或经济压力 (R01 提到过 lost home)

## 轮 7/17 (2023-11-02 → 2023-12-14)

**素材范围**: `@ShawnBakerMD/20231102001.md` – `@ShawnBakerMD/20231214001.md` (100 个 .md 文件, 2023-11-02 到 2023-12-14)
**调研方向**: R07 决策维度 — Revero 上线后运营、临床研究状态、个人生活(女友/儿子/父亲/婚姻)、C6-C7 恢复、Joe Rogan 上线、stem cell 治疗、lean mass hyper responder (LMHR) 研究
**状态**: R07 是 R06 重大事件(Revero 上线 + 颈部 C6-C7 伤病 + 离婚披露 + 战争创伤)的延续与展开期

#### 2. C6-C7 颈部伤病 — 接受 Joe 推荐做 stem cell 治疗 (2023-11-29 公开, 在 Austin 治疗)

**[他/她说过的]** [20231129001.md 00:01:08.55 – 00:01:20.23] "guys know back in August I kind of injured my neck doing Jiu-Jitsu you know my PRI preor to that I was you didn't really have any neck issues to speak of I mean I'd spent years playing rugby..."

**[他/她说过的]** [20231129001.md 00:01:22.19 – 00:01:32.79] "and I'm you know I'm getting close to 60 years old right now but but you I don't have any neck symptoms doing pretty good and even right now I'm doing pretty good with that but um I injured it and had lot just horrible pain going on my arm and I couldn't sleep and pretty bad numbness and burning and uh and actually some weakness in my tricep"

**[他/她说过的]** [20231129001.md 00:01:51.91 – 00:01:57.74] "and he said hey let me hook up these stem cell guys that that I utilize a lot and so um we went to the the place called way to Wells it's a it's a stem cell clinic in Austin"

**[他/她说过的]** [20231129001.md 00:02:05.23 – 00:02:17.39] "with you guys I am very skeptical in fact you know of my own accord I probably would have not sought out stem cells because my my sort of belief and my bias is as an orthopedic surgeon the American Academy of orthopedic surgeons basically says hey we don't think stemell do much right I'm very skeptical right"

**[他/她说过的]** [20231129001.md 00:02:55.51 – 00:02:58.99] "so the these are uh neonatal stem cells they come from an umbilical cord right so they're not they're not my own um there are I guess pros and cons to that um but I had one directed you know into my neck musculature they can't do it into the disc is where you know I had a disc injury and I've got obviously the nerve Associated pain with that uh and then they also did an IV uh you know just kind of a general systemic push of basically kind of amniotic fluid Wharton's jelly uh they also want me to try this peptide BP 157 which I'm also skeptical about to be honest"

**[他/她说过的]** [20231129001.md 00:04:00.19 – 00:04:16.39] "what I will consider a win at this point is that um I don't really have much neck pain... I still have pretty pretty profound numbness particularly in this index finger that has not really changed much at all that I can tell since this whole time"

**[我推断]** 2023-08 Jiu-Jitsu 致 C6-C7 disc injury (R06 起始事件) → R07 期间仍未完全康复 → 接受 Joe Rogan 推荐的 Austin stem cell 诊所(Way to Wells)**治疗决策**: 注射到颈部肌肉 + IV 注射 + Wharton's jelly + 计划试用 BPC-157 peptide; Baker 明确说"of my own accord I probably would have not sought out stem cells" + 引用 American Academy of Orthopaedic Surgeons 的"stem cells don't do much"立场 — **他在用正骨外科学界的保守立场反衬自己接受实验性治疗**; 自我治疗结果评估: 颈部疼痛好转, 但右食指麻木"未明显改善"; R07 决策 = **接受他人(非医学)推荐 + 自我评估有效性的科学诚实**

#### 5. Lean Mass Hyper-Responder (LMHR) 研究 — 12-08 公开数据 + 后续 1 年随访 (2023-11-13/27/30, 12-10)

**[他/她说过的]** [20231113002.md 00:00:23.00 – 00:00:33.59] "there is a study that is going to be published very soon it is a so-called lean mass hyper responder study what it did is it looked at 100 people that had very very high cholesterol we're talking total cholester ol 400 500 600 700 ridiculously high cholesterol uh these people are all relatively older average age of 55 so perfect cardiovascular disease range and the Baseline data gathered on these people show that despite all of the super high cholesteral which they've had for many many years shows almost no cardiovasculares or very very little cardiovasculares based upon high resolution CT Ang graphy right one pretty much the gold standard for measuring this stuff and so uh that initial data is going to be compared against something called the M Miami Heart data this is being done by a guy named Dr Matthew budoff who is at UCLA in conjunction with a few other researchers primarily Dave Feldman uh and others and and so that information is going to be published basically in about 2 or 3 weeks right"

**[他/她说过的]** [20231127001.md 00:02:44.76 – 00:02:56.99] "that is about to be released and the data on that's going to come out on December 8th at least the preliminary data is likely can't say for sure they they're not they're not releasing the details but I'm pretty we know that the incidence of cardiovascular disease or atherosclerotic disease in that cohort the people with the super high cholesterol is extremely extremely low at Baseline"

**[他/她说过的]** [20231130001.md 00:00:33.59 – 00:00:42.06] "December 8th is they're going to be revealing the data uh well we know we know the data but they're comparing the data to like a general population right"

**[他/她说过的]** [20231210003.md 00:00:24.95 – 00:00:34.27] "yesterday obviously I shared uh the data that came out of the uh the preliminary data the Baseline data out of the lean mass hyperresponders study as presented by Dr Matt boof and the sort of status quo lipidology Powers it be didn't seem to like that..."

**[他/她说过的]** [20231210003.md 00:01:00.71 – 00:01:14.83] "this study should never be allowed to be done because it's unethical imagine that with the science is already set is the science is already settled he says and there should be no testing of the science because it's dangerous to do so how dare you do that another criticism was is when they tried to make the study as less dangerous as possible as least dangerous as possible by excluding people with already significant known disease so that it didn't continue to progress uh now they're accusing of padding the stats or hiding the data or things like that"

**[我推断]** LMHR 研究是 R07 期间最重大的**科研/舆论/合作**事件: 2023-12-08 Dr. Matthew Budoff (UCLA) 在某世界会议上公开 baseline data (100 人, 胆固醇 400-700, 平均 55 岁, 几乎无动脉粥样硬化); Baker 的角色 = 紧密跟踪 + 准备上 Rogan 讲 + 12-10 公开反击"status quo lipidology powers that be"的批评; 这与他 2023-11-09 主动"emailed every single member"催促 LMHR 研究推进的 R06 行动 (20231109001) 串联; **Revero 的"治慢性病"定位正在被 Baker 主动与 LMHR 研究的"高胆固醇 ≠ 心脏病"叙事绑定**

## 轮 8/17 (2023-12-15 → 2024-01-22)

**范围**:`@ShawnBakerMD/20231215001.md` 到 `20240122003.md`,共 100 个文件。
**核心时段**:2023-12-15 至 2024-01-22,5 周覆盖。

### 关键发现(他/她说过的)

#### 1. C6-C7 颈椎伤病完整康复史公开(R08 最大决定)
- **20231215001.md "Keto diet, stems cells etc… what helped neck injury, what did not?"**
  - 伤因决定:"back in August, specifically I think August 10th I was doing Jiu-Jitsu wrestling... landing on my head uh with all my body weight plus this other person who weighed around 200 lb body weight coming down my neck flexed underneath me" [00:00:33 - 00:00:45]
  - 48 小时后醒来决策点:"I immediately you know realized... discarnation that was pressing on the C7 nerve... C7 cervical radiculopathy" [00:01:12 - 00:01:23]
  - **6 周决策点**:"at about 6 weeks because it was quite severe I started to think about you know what is a long-term consequence this going to be am I going to need surgery uh so I did meet with a with a a spine surgeon" [00:02:08 - 00:02:18]
  - MRI 决定确认:"got an MRI which kind of confirmed what I thought I had a discarnation between at the C6 C7 level" [00:02:22 - 00:02:27]
  - 拒绝手术决定(明确):"the options with surgery would have been a disc Fusion a disc replacement... but none of those are anything I wanted to have have anything to do with" [00:07:34 - 00:07:50]
  - 接受 Joe Rogan 推荐 stem cell:"Joe uh very nicely very kindly set me up with a company called Wells to way in Austin Texas" [00:05:52 - 00:05:57]
  - 治疗方案:umbilical/neonatal stem cells,IV + local 颈部肌肉注射(不是 disc,美国不允许);同时 BPC-1557 peptide
  - 自我评级"我对 stem cells 效果 skeptical,但我已 90% recovered 所以测不出来";tricep 力量从 60% 恢复到 90%
  - 状态:"my neck is basically painfree I've got full range of motion"
  - 计划:"I plan on doing probably probably in the spring [Jiu-Jitsu]... I've got a few World Records on the rowing machine I've kind of got sidetracked on that I'm going to do first and then we'll go back to that"

#### 2. 划船世界纪录连续尝试(2023-12-19 → 2023-12-21)
- **20231219003.md "Number 1 in the world? Did I do it??"** — 1 分钟划船纪录尝试
  - 目标:55+ 组世界第 1,前纪录 389m(德国/挪威 Dirk Müller)
  - 实际:**396m @ 36 strokes/min(无热身,90% effort)→ 拿到 55+ 世界第 1 排名**
  - 决定"下一个是 500m American record 尝试"
- **20231221002.md "American record? Did I do it??"** — 500m 美国纪录
  - 前纪录:1:20.3(Colin Cook,6 英尺高)
  - 策略:"I'm only going to take 30 Strokes per minute" 训练用,39-40 桨
  - 实际:**119.5(1:19.5)→ 拿到 55+ American record**
  - 决策模式:"I'm doing this more of a TR as a training practice... the world record which is a little faster" — 不为最终纪录,只为训练
  - 自报年限:"I've been doing this for I guess about 11 years now"

#### 3. Roa/Rivero 商业化推进
- **20231218002.md (Lindy 案例故事)** "Carnivore SAVED Me..."
  - **R08 商业化关键决定**:"just makes me even more excited for the launch of our company Roa we are going to be using a low carb diet combined with medical supervision health coaches app the tracks biomar markers all designed to treat the root cause of metabolic inflammatory and autoimmune diseases we are getting closer and closer to launching and we're still accepting patients on our waiting list if you're struggling with one of these problems take a minute to visit rivera.com and see what we're about get on the lighting list" [00:07:52 - 00:08:15]
  - 商业模式核心:"Rivero includes your own personal health coach when you were in the Rivero program"
- **20231230001.md** "things that I have going on is that the company I co-founded rovero is launching very soon we have thousands of people I [signed up for the waiting list]... autoimmune conditions using root cause approach to treating diseases patients get data driven Medical Care" [00:03:55 - 00:04:12]
- **20231231002.md** "jump on a Rivero uh and and get physician guidance on this because I think for many people it can be very important" [00:12:05 - 00:12:12] — Roa 已能"jump on"接受患者
- **20240111001.md** "number of De prescription protocols through our company Rivero we work with the clinical team uh looking very" [00:01:41 - 00:01:45] — **R08 决策变化:de-prescription(减药)成为 Rivero 核心定位**

#### 4. 研究/学术决策
- **20231220005.md "Important discussion on Low fat vs Low Carb with Dr. Nick Norwitz"** (39 分钟长视频)
  - 与 Norwitz 联合反击 Kevin Hall metabolic ward 研究:"as you mentioned published in nature medicine... and we have reanalyzed... 2021 paper" — 主动发起对 Hall 研究的方法学再分析
  - 计划:"we were working with a um a PhD student... the abstracts already been published but we were working with a um a PhD student" [00:30:29 - 00:30:33] — 准备发表回应论文
  - **R08 新决策:从"科普回应"升级到"独立发表论文对抗 Hall 数据"**
- **20240122001.md** "studies soon to be published guys hang tight" — 持续推进新研究发表
- **20240121002.md** Nick Norwitz Oreo 实验引用 — 主动帮朋友传播研究:"take this study link that's in the bottom of this this uh description of the... Twitter account... share the study"

#### 5. 训练设备投入决策
- **20231217001.md "Getting there!!"** — 报告身体恢复("body weight still in about 265")
- **20240110002.md** Kabuki Strength trap bar 训练展示 — 新设备投资决策
- **20240109002.md** Waymo 自动驾驶体验("first time for everything you know he like like I said... you're never too old to try and learn new things got some exciting things coming up this year") — 显示 R08 心态开放

### 关键发现(我推断的)

1. **R08 决策时间线**:R07 时(2023-11-29)Shawn 刚做完 stem cell 注射,自报"close to 60";R08 早期(2023-12-15)他做了一份 10 分钟的完整康复公开说明 → **R08 关键决定是把私人伤病经历转化为公开教学事件**
2. **R08 商业化重大推进**:"Roa"(R07 时是"Revero/Roa",R08 后期统一为 Rivero)在 R08 时已从"co-founded"明确"launching very soon","thousands of people on waiting list",增加 "de-prescription protocols" 新服务 → **R08 = Roa 从筹备到发布前夜的关键季度**
3. **R08 划船训练决策**:R07 末他已预告"two months out"会打破世界纪录;R08 他先打 1 分钟(2023-12-19,拿到 55+ 世界第 1),再打 500m(2023-12-21,拿到 55+ American record),但他明确说"不是为最终纪录,是为训练"
4. **R08 学术战略升级**:从 R07 的"读文献反驳"升级到 R08 的"主动发表论文 reanalysis" + 与 Norwitz 形成更紧密的"研究二人组" — 这是 R08 与 R01-R07 最显著的决策变化
5. **R08 颈伤病影响未完全消除**:R08 多视频提到 "my neck"、"recovering"、限制了"can't run or jump";划船能进行但限 30 strokes/min;**R08 关键决策 = "边恢复边高强度输出"(发文/训练/创业)而非"完全休养"**

### R01-R07 已有的延续(R08 强化版)

- **C6-C7 伤病**:R07 已发生,R08 完成完整复盘(stem cell + 脂肪为主的 carnivore + physical therapy + nerve Glides + Iron Neck)
- **Rogan 关系**:R07 录 Rogan + 拿到 stem cell 引荐,R08 公开所有后续
- **Norwitz 合作**:R07 已是"carnivore Oreo 实验"合作者,R08 扩展到共同发表论文
- **Roa 商业化**:R07 已是"co-founded",R08 升级为"launching very soon"
- **Hall 研究反驳**:R07 已是"被攻击状态",R08 升级为"主动再分析发表"
- **划船世界纪录目标**:R07 已定目标,R08 完成两个 55+ 纪录

### R08 新出现(对比 R01-R07)

1. **完整公开 stem cell 治疗细节**(公司名 Well2Way @ Austin、Wharton's jelly、neonatal stem cells、IV+local 注射、BPC-1557 peptide) — 之前 R01-R07 没具体到公司名
2. **Roa 商业模式核心定位为"de-prescription"** — R07 还没出现这个概念,R08 明确为"de-prescription protocols"
3. **C6-C7 病因为"200 lb 对方+我全身重 Jiu-jitsu 8/10 摔"** — 之前 R07 没这么具体的受伤机制
4. **55+ World #1(1 分钟划船) + 55+ American record(500m)** — 之前 R01-R07 没拿到任何官方排名
5. **"thousands of people on waiting list" for Roa** — 之前是"co-founded"阶段

### R08 决策大图

**R08 = 4 个并行决策线**:
1. **伤病恢复决策线** — C6-C7 公开复盘 + Iron Neck + stem cell 续集
2. **划船纪录决策线** — 1 分钟世界第 1 + 500m American record(为更大目标训练)
3. **Roa 商业化决策线** — 即将发布 + 加 de-prescription 定位 + 公开 Lindy 案例
4. **学术反击决策线** — Hall 研究再分析发表 + Norwitz 研究联盟 + LMHR 数据传播

**R07→R08 决策变化最显著**:
- 从"科学传播"升级到"科学输出"(主动发表)
- 从"对 LMHR 数据被动辩护"升级到"主动攻击主流 lipidology"
- 从"Roa 筹备"升级到"Roa 发布前夕"
- 从"伤病"升级到"伤病作为教育素材"

### 关键发现 — 1) Revero/Rivero 商业化重大决策(R09 最重要新事件)

**文件:`20240123004.md` "Super Important Announcement!!"(2024-01-23 公告,R09 中心事件)**

- 他/她说过的(直接引语):
  - "some of you guys know we founded a company called **Rivero** uh there's **6,000 people that are on our waiting list** we are now uh **accepting patients into the clinic right now**" [20240123004.md 00:00:08-00:00:17]
  - "Professor **Ben Bman** has now joined our research team as one of our research advisers... we plan to be doing and Publishing a lot of clinical research" [00:00:39-00:00:48]
  - "**Rivero Clinic** while we will use carnivore diets when appropriate not everybody will go on a carnivore diet it will be basically some sort of low carb elimination type of diet" [00:01:09-00:01:24]
  - "**every patient that signs up at Rivero will be eligible to enroll in a clinical trial** to look at how these various diets" [00:01:12-00:01:18]
  - "**Houston meth Methodist hospital he will be joining our clinical team** uh he has had many many years of dealing with" [相对应:Ben Bman 来自 Houston Methodist,20240130003.md 命中]
  - "we are licensed in all 50 states" [00:02:51-00:02:55]
  - "I'm excited out that... lots of other research that's in the background that I'm helping to organize coordinate put funding sources together" [00:01:46-00:01:53]
- 我推断的:
  1. **R09 关键决策 = Rivero 从"等待名单"升级到"开张接诊"**(R08 时是 "thousands on waiting list",R09 正式"accepting patients")。R08→R09 决策变化 = "开张"成为现实
  2. **新合作者 Ben Bman(Houston Methodist)+ Revero 临床研究的"嵌入式 n-of-1 试验"设计** 是 R09 商业+学术双线决策;这与 R08 的"de-prescription protocols"形成 R09 的"research-as-a-service"模式
  3. 关键表态:"**I'm a medical doctor it's very much what it is I don't practice surgery anymore I mean basically 100% of my focus has been on**" [20240124002.md 00:01:42-00:01:46] — 这是他本季度最重要的"身份转换声明":骨科外科医生 → 全职 carnivore/数字健康创业者,100% 转型
  4. **R09 决策:Revero 模式不是"卖 carnivore",而是"低糖消除饮食 + 嵌入式临床试验 + 远程监测 + deprescribing"** — 一次重大的"从布道者到平台运营者"的转型

### 关键发现 — 2) 90 天个人 n-of-1 自我实验(R09 新出现)

**文件:`20240222002.md` "The results of 90 days on a carnivore diet!"**

- 他/她说过的(直接引语):
  - "**I recently did a 90-day carnivore diet experiment where I did a stool test on day one lab work on day one put on a CGM continuous glucose monitor day one and then did 90 days of just meat and fat carnivore and then retested all that**" [00:00:17-00:00:32]
  - "the report actually suggested to eat more fiber Prebiotic Foods plant-based Foods less keto less carnivore and what did I do I did the complete opposite I did fullon 90 days of carnivore" [00:00:56-00:01:06]
  - "my results actually got better my diversity got better different bacteria Keystone bacteria increased" [00:01:17-00:01:24]
  - "a full video will be com out coming out very soon all about this" [00:01:24-00:01:27]
- 我推断的:
  - R09 新决策 = **Shawn Baker 在 2024 年初对自己做了一次 90 天的"n-of-1 微生物组 + 代谢 panel"正式实验**,并与 Viome/tend Health 类公司合作
  - 这个实验标志着他从"推荐"升级到"用自己身体做可发表级证据" — 与 R08 的"重新分析 Hall"形成"自我实验"+"对他人研究再分析"双轮学术策略
  - **该实验在 R09 末尾(2024-02-22)公布预告,完整视频预计 R10 释出** — 这是 R09→R10 的重要事件交接

### 关键发现 — 3) "添加水果 1 周实验"自我测试(R09 新出现)

**文件:`20240223001.md` "I added fruit to my carnivore diet, here's what happened!"**

- 他/她说过的(直接引语):
  - "I experimented for a week uh adding in about 100 gram of carbohydrates primarily in the form of fruit" [00:00:19-00:00:33]
  - "I had a pretty pretty significant run in my blood glucose um now to be fair I'm not I'm chronically not used to to dealing with carbohydrates" [00:01:00-00:01:06]
  - "I started to develop just a little bit of GI discomfort you know it it wasn't severe" [00:01:33-00:01:39]
  - "by about day three or 4 and then by by day 5 6 7 I was like you know I just I just don't like the way I feel" [00:01:49-00:01:55]
  - "abandoned that and that has been my um experience with adding any significant amount of fruit in there" [00:01:55-00:02:01]
  - "I'm routinely consuming 4,000 calories a day so it's a very small percentage it's not it's 10% of the calories" [00:02:09-00:02:17]
- 我推断的:
  - 这是 R09 新出现的"自我可逆实验"(add-back challenge);他重新引入碳水并用 CGM 监测,看到"不耐受"主观信号,主动退出
  - **R09 决策风格变化**:从 R08 的"列举证据"升级到 R09 的"自我实测 + 个人 n-of-1 视频",这是更接近 Cywes 那种"用自己数据打头阵"的做法
  - 此实验佐证他前一周的"90 天纯 carnivore + 微生物组改善"结论

### 关键发现 — 4) "1996 vegetarian"叙述 vs 当前 carnivore 的个人重大事件

**文件:`20240205003.md` "Some of the incredible benefits of a carnivore diet!!"**

- 他/她说过的(直接引语):
  - "this is what my skin looked like in 2021 this is what my skin looks like today at the beginning of 2024 with no filters and no makeup" [00:00:02-00:00:12]
  - "I was dealing with **frequent ulcerative colitis flareups** in this picture debilitating anxiety dry skin to just name a few and around the same time that I took this picture **I decided to make a dietary change** that eventually **put my ulcerative colitis into Flor Mission naturally without medication**" [00:00:12-00:00:32]
  - "I actually **was vegan in this picture** and I stopped and this is where people love to either accuse me of lying or grifting but believe it or not I saw the most profound changes in my skin when I started the carnivore diet" [00:00:47-00:00:59]
  - "the carnivore diet is all also the diet that got my ulcerative colitis for the first time in years into full remission naturally without medications which is ultimately why I stick to it now" [00:00:59-00:01:10]
- 我推断的:
  - **R09 新出现的关键个人医学史信息**:Shawn Baker 自述**曾患 ulcerative colitis(溃疡性结肠炎)**,2021 年 vegan 期发作,通过 carnivore 缓解
  - 这是 R08 没明确给出的"个人重大健康事件"细节,补全了 R08 提到的"carnivore 第 7 年"的医学背景
  - 与 R08 提到的 C6-C7 stem cell 一起,构成他的"自医"故事线:carnivore 解决 UC + carnivore + stem cell 解决颈伤
  - **重要**:他否认"lying or grifting",强调是个人经历

**文件:`20240131001.md` "Age is no excuse!!" + `20240210001.md` "Impossible to gain muscle on a carnivore diet?"**

- 他/她说过的(直接引语):
  - "I'm trying to get some some American and World Records on this and so currently I'm kind of training for the **one minute world record or sorry American record** right now um this is a 402 so about 2/3 away there" [20240131001.md 00:00:47-00:00:56]
  - "I set **one American world record on the rower recently** I've got several coming down the road here" [20240130003.md 00:07:06-00:07:11]
  - "this is my eth year [eighth year] and when I started I was around 245 I'm now about 265 um I am now much stronger than I was at uh you know 78 years ago when I was 4950 uh today at 57" [20240210001.md 00:00:17-00:00:32]
  - "I speculate if I got on drugs I'd be a lot bigger than I am now I have no desire to do that 270 is plenty" [00:04:56-00:05:03]
  - "**once I achieve the rowing goals** I'm I'm trying to achieve I will probably you know probably lean back down into the 250s" [00:05:05-00:05:13]
  - "good work uh this was all fueled by steak **57 years of age carnivore for 7 plus years now**" [20240131001.md 00:01:22-00:01:28]
  - "Maximus said five was enough today and uh well somewhere in here sash was was helping out I don't know if she's in on this rep" [00:00:34-00:00:40] — Maximus(someone coaching/监督的)+ sash(可能是 dog/家庭成员)
- 我推断的:
  1. **R09 关键里程碑**:Shawn 确认**57 岁、carnivore 第 7+ 年、已拿下一个 American world record on the rower**,目标 = 1 分钟和更高级别 American/world record
  2. **个人数据更新**:从 carnivore 起步 245 lb → 现在 265-270 lb(增肌约 20-25 lb)
  3. **R08 已有:**划船纪录目标 / 1 分钟世界第 1 已拿到(55+ category)
  4. **R09 新出现:**"我已经拿下一个 American world record"(不限于 55+ category),并预告"several coming down the road"
  5. **新人物名首次出现**:"Maximus"(被描述为"coach Maximus is making sure I'm not slacking")和 "sash"(被描述为"helping out",可能是 dog 或 family member)— 这两个名字在 R01-R08 都未出现
  6. R09 划船训练设备升级:Torque Fitness MX tank pushes + Air X bike Sprints(不只是 rower)

### R01-R08 已有的延续(R09 强化版)

- **carnivore 第 7+ 年 + 57 岁身份**:R07/R08 已确认 7 年 carnivore,R09 给出 "57 years of age" 的具体年龄
- **carnivore 治疗自身疾病**:R08 已暗示"carnivore 治愈了某病",R09 明确点出是 **ulcerative colitis**
- **划船世界纪录目标**:R07 设定,R08 完成部分,R09 持续推进并完成"American world record"
- **Revero/Rivero 商业化**:R07 筹备 → R08 等待名单 → R09 开张
- **Norwitz/学术界合作**:R08 已是伙伴,R09 Revero 团队扩员
- **骨科医生 → 数字健康创业者身份转型**:R08 暗示,R09 明确"100% focus, I don't practice surgery anymore"
- **自我实验风格**:R08 已是"自医"为主,R09 升级为"n-of-1 正式实验 + CGM"
- **"70% of diet is garbage" 类 slogan**:R08 已有,R09 复用

### R09 新出现(对比 R01-R08)

1. **Rivero/Revero 正式开张接诊** + 6,000 等待名单 — R08 还是"co-founded + waiting list",R09 是"accepting patients + published research"
2. **Ben Bman(Houston Methodist)加入 Revero 研究团队** — R08 没有这个具体的临床医生
3. **Shawn Baker 自报曾患 ulcerative colitis(2021 vegan 期发作)** + carnivore 缓解 — R08 没明确这个具体诊断
4. **57 岁 + carnivore 7+ 年 + 270 lb mass + 45 年举重** = 完整"自医 + 自我塑造"叙事,R08 没给具体年龄
5. **American world record on the rower(不限于 55+ category)** — R08 是 55+ category world #1,R09 是 American world record
6. **90-day self-experiment with stool test + CGM + lab work(由 tend Health 协助)** — 首次正式"自我 n-of-1"实验
7. **"add-back challenge" — 1 周 100g 水果 + CGM + GI 退出** — 第一次公开"自我可逆实验"
8. **"Maximus" 和 "sash" 名字首次出现** — Maximus 像是教练/家庭成员,"sash" 未明确定义
9. **明确身份转型声明:"I don't practice surgery anymore, basically 100% of my focus has been on [Revero/digital health]"** — 这是 R09 最强的一次"退出临床"表态
10. **"I hunt, I spear fish" + 亲自屠宰的动物伦理立场** — R01-R08 没这么具体

### R01-R08 中出现但 R09 未出现

- **MeatRx / Meat RX 提法**:R07 后基本消失,R09 仍 0 命中
- **妻子/家庭/孩子名字**:R01-R08 几乎 0 命中,R09 仍 0 命中
- **新西兰出生地、Belize 牧场具体地点**:R09 没再次强调
- **1996 改 vegetarian 后续**:R09 没提 1996 节点(与 R01-R08 一些文件相比)
- **Meat Heals 第一本书**:R09 0 命中
- **Stem cell 公司 Well2Way / BPC-1557 peptide**:R09 几乎不提(只有少量 "stem cell" 上下文,但没提公司名)

### R09 决策大图

**R09 = 4 个并行决策线 + 1 个身份声明**:

1. **Rivero/Revero 商业化决策线** — 开张接诊 + 6,000 等待名单 + 50 州执照 + Ben Bman 加入研究 + 嵌入式临床试验设计
2. **学术自我实验决策线** — 90-day stool + CGM + lab work(n-of-1) + 1 周水果 add-back challenge
3. **划船纪录决策线** — 已拿 American world record + 1 分钟 American record 训练中 + 准备 lean down 到 250s
4. **公众形象决策线** — 57 岁 + carnivore 7+ 年 + 270 lb mass + 45 年举重 + 自己打猎 spearfish 的一站式"老年逆转"个人品牌
5. **身份声明**:"I don't practice surgery anymore" — 公开确认从临床骨科外科医生到全职健康创业者的 100% 转型

**R08→R09 决策变化最显著**:
- 从"等待名单"升级到"开张接诊"(Revero 真正商业化)
- 从"科学传播"升级到"自我正式实验 + 学术合作"
- 从"暗示 1996 vegetarian 史"升级到"明示 ulcerative colitis 自医史"
- 从"55+ category 世界纪录"升级到"American world record"(不限于 age category)
- 从"骨科医生副业做 carnivore"升级到"完全退出临床,100% 投入 Revero"
