## 轮 10/17 (2024-02-25 → 2024-04-03)

### 调研元数据

- 素材范围: `@ShawnBakerMD/20240225002.md` – `@ShawnBakerMD/20240403001.md`(100 个 .md 文件,按文件名升序对应素材池第 901-1000 位)
- 来源: YouTube auto-caption 直链下载(`caption_source: youtube_auto_caption_direct_download`),全部 `review_status: direct_download_unreviewed`
- 调研方向: 重大决策 / 个人生活事件 / 与主张强相关的行动;R10 vs R01-R09 的变化点

### 关键 grep 命令

| 命令 | 命中文件数 |
|------|----------|
| `grep -iE "I did\|I decided\|I started\|I wrote\|my book\|I quit\|I changed\|I stopped\|became\|career\|orthopedic\|revero\|clinic\|company\|MeatRx\|launched"` | 30 |
| `grep -iE "personal\|family\|wife\|kid\|son\|father\|dad\|mom\|mother\|girlfriend\|married\|wedding\|divorce\|children\|grew up"` | 39 |
| `grep -iE "rowing\|world record\|athletic\|row\|ran\|race\|racing\|marathon\|triathlon\|basketball\|track\|weight lift\|jiu jitsu\|BJJ\|deadlift\|squat\|trap bar\|neck\|disc\|injury\|stem cell\|hunt\|spear"` | 31 |
| `grep -iE "revero\|meatrx\|meatheals\|coaching\|coach"` | 2 (核心 20240312001 / 20240301002) |
| `grep -iE "research\|study\|trial\|clinical\|publish\|peer review\|paper\|journal"` | 全部 100 (此轮"做研究"成主题) |
| `grep -iE "podcast\|interview\|guest\|Joe Rogan\|Huberman"` | 6 (20240228003 / 20240318002 / 20240322001 / 20240326003 / 20240327001) |
| `grep -iE "my son\|my daughter\|my wife\|my father\|my mom\|my mother\|my brother\|my girlfriend"` | 2 (`my mom` 1 引用为他人话语;`my girlfriend` 1 = 20240301001 自己的) |
| `grep -iE "deadlift\|world record\|rowing\|Concept 2\|stem cell"` | 9 (rowing/世界纪录核心:20240225003/20240303001/20240304003/20240309002/20240319003/20240326003) |

### 关键发现 (R10,按决策量级排序)

#### 1. 【R10 新-延续 R09】公开宣称同时主导多项 carnivore 临床研究 + Rivero 数据论文,角色已从"我是个体观察者"转为"我在做科研基础设施 / 同时跑独立项目"

**[他/她说过的]** [20240326003.md 00:02:55 – 00:03:38] "I'm excited to get some research done you know uh we'll be doing research I'm doing it independent I'm also doing it within the company of Rivero we're going to be doing publishing research on our patient data uh uh but but I'm also trying to get this broad research done… people often accuse me of being not scientific or just depending on anecdotes believe me it's not for lack of trying I've been trying this for a long long time finally finally I'm starting to get some funding together with researchers that seem like it's going to get off the ground and we'll have hopefully some more studies out there"

**[他/她说过的]** [20240326003.md 00:00:05 – 00:00:23] "I just got out of a research meeting uh working with one of the state legislators hopefully to fund a study that is basically carnivore and its effect on health uh you know I'm in the background you know putting in the work…"

**注**:R10 新出现"与州立法者合作申请研究经费"+ "Rivero 在准备发病人数据论文";R01-R09 主要是抱怨"没人做 RCT";R10 是公开宣布"我正在做基础设施级动作"。

#### 2. 【R10 新】Revero 在快速扩 50 州临床团队 + 公开欢迎新临床医师加入

**[他/她说过的]** [20240402002.md 00:00:08 – 00:00:39] "before I discuss that I'd like to just announce uh welcoming yet another clinician to our Rivera practice this time uh this is Julie Kelly who has had many many years practicing uh metabolic Health improvements… she is licensed in I believe 10 states she's based out of Oklahoma licens in Texas uh Colorado Arizona Montana Washington Florida Iowa and a couple others I can't remember"

**[他/她说过的]** [20240402002.md 00:02:13 – 00:02:32] "I'm super excited about Rivera for you guys that are on the waiting list we're getting there we're you know we're scaling to as many states as possible we have uh you know we have basically licens in all 50 states but we just need to get all our providers there which we are rapidly uh doing we have another you know several other we're bringing on this week"

**[他/她说过的]** [20240312001.md 00:00:42 – 00:01:05] "we have been able to see people come off the medications to no longer need the drugs and to put those diseases in remission by using lifestyle and nutrition this is what Rivera does if you know somebody with one of those diseases and you're interested and have Physicians that understand this come check us out at rivera.com"

**注**:R09/R08 已提 Revero 平台,R10 首次出现"50 州牌照 + 提名具体新加入医师 + 等待名单"等运营细节,表明 Revero 从"理念"进入"明显招聘扩张期"。

#### 3. 【R10 新】公开自我实验:连续 6 周纯 steak + eggs,先短期加苹果做胆固醇实验,再回到 99% carnivore + 体重从 ~265 lb 降到 ~263 lb

**[他/她说过的]** [20240318002.md 00:00:18 – 00:00:54] "about gosh six weeks ago I did a little sort of short-term experiment to add fruit in my diet uh for the express purpose to see what impact it had on my cholesterol given the uh sort of data around lean mass hyperresponders and so I did add uh you know 100 G of basically apples a day for for roughly one week um it had minimal to no impact on my cholesterol"

**[他/她说过的]** [20240318002.md 00:02:00 – 00:02:25] "I am pretty much 99% carnivore you know and I've said on many occasions even on Roga that occasionally I'll have like a piece of cake on a birthday once in a while… fact I've been eating nothing but you know steak and eggs for the last 6 weeks I feel great on that"

**[他/她说过的]** [20240319003.md 00:00:28 – 00:00:36] "weight is I'm coming down and weight a little bit so I'm down around 260 maybe 263 is so uh still the uh the strength is still pretty good… I'm focused on rowing so there's some specific things I want to accomplish here"

**[他/她说过的]** [20240225003.md 标题及] "My cholesterol more than doubled in under 24 hours!!" — 公开演示自己 37 小时禁食 + 1 小时有氧后总胆固醇从 156 →346 mg/dL 的自我实验。

**注**:R10 出现首次明确的"我目前 99% carnivore + 偶尔生日蛋糕"的自我立场更新,以及第一次明确的"短期加水果做胆固醇响应实验"——n-of-1 实验从"经验之谈"升级为"对外公开的实验日记"。

#### 4. 【R10 新】打破 50-59 岁组 100 米划船世界纪录(13.8 / 13.9 秒);自称这是"另一个世界纪录"——R10 期间(2024-03-09 → 2024-03-25)至少两次世界纪录尝试

**[他/她说过的]** [20240309002.md 00:00:02 – 00:00:33] "on the road for a quick attempt at the 100 meter world record for the 50 to 59 class the world record currently is 13.9 seconds so here I go here hoping to hit this and uh funny thing is the register said 13.8 when I was done and so I was pretty happy uh you know to set a new world record… yes I got the world record 138 but no the computer said 13.9 and so I am number one in the world tied the world record again uh you know I got to do better than that and then 13th overall in the world for all age groups"

**[他/她说过的]** [20240326003.md 00:00:23 – 00:00:36] "you know yesterday I broke another world record on a rowing machine again I put up results"

**[他/她说过的]** [20240318002.md 00:01:30 – 00:01:32] "additionally 100 G for someone my size at 26 60 some lbs may not be enough" (自报体重 260+ lb)

**[他/她说过的]** [20240301001.md 标题 + 00:00:59 – 00:01:11] "Plating a 1-year olds breakfast 👀" + "this plant protein thing again that I tried yesterday today she didn't like it just flat out alone um I think a couple people suggested to do it in smoothies that's what my girlfriend said"

**[他/她说过的]** [20240309002.md 00:02:58 – 00:03:08] "between having a family and and you know being a founder of a company and doing social media and also trying to be an athlete and traveling and speaking I just don't have a lot of extra time guys"

#### 10. 【R10 新】Lipidology 圈对他自我实验(37h 禁食 + 1h 有氧 → 胆固醇 156→346 mg/dL)的反驳,他公开二次回应

**[他/她说过的]** [20240227003.md 00:00:01 – 00:01:18] "I had checked my cholesterol over two subsequent uh about 15 hour periods and and the first time it was 156 milligrams per deer… and the second time was 346 it went up 190 points in about about 14 hours which is shows the the extreme Dynamic variability of cholesterol… this lipidologist says something which I think is completely it shows a complet complete lack of insight into human biology"

**注**:R10 出现明确的"用自身做胆固醇极端波动实验 → 接收来自 lipidologist 的反驳 → 公开二次回应"的完整循环。R01-R09 已有"为自己辩护"的姿态,R10 把它升级为"用自己身体做对照实验来挑战临床共识"的方法论。

### R10 vs R01-R09 决策变化总结

| 维度 | R01-R09 | R10 |
|---|---|---|
| 临床研究身份 | "找不到人做研究" / 零散提"想做" | **首次公开**:已与州立法者合作申请经费;Rivero 在准备数据论文 |
| Revero 阶段 | 平台理念 / 推广 | **运营扩张期**:50 州牌照 + 公开欢迎新加入医师 + 等待名单 |
| n-of-1 实验 | 经验之谈,模糊 | **n-of-1 实验日记化**:短期水果加入 / 6 周纯 steak+eggs / 37h 禁食 + 1h 有氧后胆固醇 156→346 |
| 训练身份 | 通用"我练举重 / 划船" | **明确化**:50-59 组 100m 划船世界纪录(13.8 / 13.9s)、deadlift 405×15 warm-up、bulking phase ~260-263 lb |
| 家庭叙事 | 模糊"family" | **首次具体**:1 岁女儿、"girlfriend"(非 wife)、"founder + athlete + speaker + family man" 四角色 |
| 被压制叙事 | 模糊"被封" | **首次精确**:2021 COVID YT 封号 5 次 + Twitter 终身封号(后恢复) |
| 与同行的关系 | 自己 host podcast | **被动应对**:被 Thomas DeLauer 采访后标题被歪曲;明确表态"从未听过 Huberman" |
| 外科医生身份 | 主动叙述"I no longer practice surgery" | **不再主动声明**:身份重心移至"founder + athlete + family man" |

## 轮 11/17 (2024-04-03 → 2024-05-10)

### 调研元数据

- 素材范围: `@ShawnBakerMD/20240403002.md` – `@ShawnBakerMD/20240510001.md` (100 个 .md 文件)
- 确认命令: `ls | sort | sed -n '1001,1100p' | wc -l` = 100
- 来源: 同前 (YouTube auto-caption)
- 关键命题: Rivero 商业化、临床研究状态、个人生活、长期目标设定

### 关键 grep 命令 (本轮)

|---|---|---|
| `grep -ciE "I did\|I decided\|I started\|I wrote\|my book\|I quit\|I changed\|I stopped\|became\|career\|orthopedic\|rivero\|clinic\|MeatRx\|launched\|company"` | 高: `20240421003.md`(12) `20240425001.md`(10) `20240423001.md`(10) `20240422003.md`(10) `20240419002.md`(6) | Rivero / 商业化 / 医执照集中出现 |
| `grep -ciE "wife\|kid\|mother\|father\|son\|girlfriend\|married\|wedding\|divorce\|children\|family"` | 高: `20240506003.md`(28) `20240404001.md`(16) `20240412001.md`(12) `20240426002.md`(10) | 家庭叙事集中 |
| `grep -ciE "rowing\|world record\|athletic\|row\|ran\|race\|racing\|marathon\|triathlon\|basketball\|jiu jitsu\|BJJ\|deadlift\|squat\|trap bar\|neck\|disc\|injury\|surgery\|stem cell\|hunt\|spear\|ranch"` | 高: `20240421003.md`(21) `20240411001.md`(18) `20240508003.md`(15) | 运动目标集中 |

### 关键发现 (按"决策量级"排序)

#### 1. **Rivero 商业模式明确化:不做长期患者,治愈即退出** (R11 新决策表态)

**[他/她说过的]** [20240417002.md 00:03:55 – 00:04:00] "I don't want patients to hang around for years we want to get you better so that you can do these next level things… Rivero as a mechanism to get people healthy I mean we don't want them honestly I don't want patients to hang around for years"

**[他/她说过的]** [20240417002.md 00:04:32] "no one no one goes to their grave saying I wish I would have watched more TV"

**[他/她说过的]** [20240419002.md 00:05:20] "you know at Rivero we're we're helping people facilitate that to figure that out and my goal is for our Rivero patients to figure out how to get healthy to get healthy and then stay the hell out of the Health Care System"

**注**:这与 R10 的"50 州扩张 + 等待名单"形成对比 — R11 把 Rivero 的 **使命哲学** 说透:"治愈即退出",这与"AI 取代医生开药"(他 5/2 视频描述的对照场景)形成他个人立场的对立项。

#### 2. **Rivero 数据收集+ rivera.com 公开 URL + AI 医学批判框架** (R11 新出现)

**[他/她说过的]** [20240418005.md 00:00:58] "we do at Rivera we'll be collecting data on these types of things figuring out what foods differentially affect which people and which condition… check out rivera.com"

**[他/她说过的]** [20240502001.md 00:01:30 – 00:01:50] "we will see that increasingly more Physicians will be replaced with automation with AI with uh you know you can log in from your home and stick your finger in a little blood sampling device and and basically have the AI diagnosed you and have lab values done right away… many Physicians will be put out of work…"

**[他/她说过的]** [20240422003.md 00:03:28] "I much prefer what I'm doing now I much prefer uh uh the the the the the joy I receive… blazing some trails and changing the way we uh we think about Healthcare and uh I hope that honestly I hope we're wildly successful I hope our company is is a huge huge company"

**注**:R10 提"准备数据论文",R11 提"collecting data" — Rivero 在 R11 阶段从"扩张"演变为"数据收集 + 公开 URL 宣示 + 公开押注公司赌注(hope hugely successful)"。

#### 3. **年龄 57 (差半岁 58) → 设 60 岁 5 大体能目标** (R11 新里程碑)

**[他/她说过的]** [20240411001.md 00:00:08 – 00:01:20] "I'm 57 nearly 57 and a half and so it gives me about two years to hit these goals… a 600 pound deadlift… dunking a basketball… sub six minute mile… sub 60 second 400 meter… sub 13 second 100 meter… I'm 6'5 so that's obviously an advantage there"

**[他/她说过的]** [20240425001.md 00:00:20] "I'm 57 years of age 6'5 by 260 I started out a little rowing here again 45 seconds uh average 111.3 honestly on this this is in preparation for a one minute world record or American record which is probably the hardest the American record stands at 403 M I think I'll get that pretty easily it's my record"

**[他/她说过的]** [20240421003.md 00:00:51] "I'm now 57 almost 57 and a half been doing this diet for just a little over longer than seven and a half years"

**[他/她说过的]** [20240426002.md 00:00:15] "yesterday I broke another American record on the rowing machine… I've been on this carnivore diet for now eight years still performing at a high level"

**[他/她说过的]** [20240417002.md 00:03:00] "I'm currently weigh in this morning right at 260 lbs I'll probably have to get down to probably 240 maybe even sub 240 to to to be able to do that realistically"

**[他/她说过的]** [20240411001.md 00:03:30 – 00:03:40] "obviously I have to eat well obviously I have to sleep well obviously I have to train efficiently and avoid injury"

**注**:R01-R10 已知他"50 岁前后密集打破划船世界纪录",R11 是**首次公开"60 岁 5 大目标"系统化** — 5 项目标覆盖力量(600lb deadlift)+ 爆发力(dunk)+ 速度(13s 100m / 60s 400m / 6min mile),他在公开视频里称之为"put up or shut up"承诺。

#### 4. **1996 节点首次具象:内布拉斯加 USA Powerlifting 赛,首次 deadlift 700lb 拿"Only the Strong Shall Survive" shirt** (R11 新出现,填补 R01-R10 空白)

**[他/她说过的]** [20240427005.md 00:00:12 – 00:00:34] "this is a shirt I got way back in 1996 I remember I got this in Nebraska at a USA powerlifting meet the first time I had deadlifted uh 700 lbs of course I was very proud that I had done that as a drug-free athlete and I you know I've had to shirt for gosh what is that almost almost 30 years now still still fits okay I was a little bigger when I first got I was closer to 280 now I'm down to about 260"

**[他/她说过的]** [20240427005.md 00:00:20] "it says Only the Strong shall survive and I can stand up high enough and they shall be drug free"

**[他/她说过的]** [20240427005.md 00:05:54] "I'm very proud of the fact that I've been a drug-free athlete my entire life"

**注**:R01-R10 多次提"drug-free my whole life"但 0 命中具体年份。R11 锁定 **1996 年 = Nebraska USA Powerlifting Meet = 首次 700lb deadlift**。这与 R01 推断的"2017 弃照前 20 年外科医生经历"对得上(1996 大约是他医学生/住院医阶段)。

#### 5. **"执照可恢复" + "我不上班" 进一步细化** (R11 延续 R01,新增"训练 3-4 个月可回去"细节)

**[他/她说过的]** [20240421003.md 00:04:57 – 00:05:09] "I could very easily go back to practicing medicine as a surgeon I have I have an active medical license I would have to you know train with somebody for you know 3 4 months just to get back to the the groove that it is and and I could very easily go back to making 6 7 $800,000 a year but I don't do that"

**[他/她说过的]** [20240421003.md 00:05:15] "I choose not to do that why one because I don't think I would enjoy it anymore and number two I much prefer what I'm doing now"

**注**:R01-R10 都提"active medical license"(2019-06-03 New Mexico),R11 **首次给出具体"回去所需的训练时长 3-4 个月"** + 明确收入对比 **$600k-$800k/年**,并在伦理层面说明"我也可以卖补剂赚更多但我不做"。

#### 6. **家庭叙事新增:妻子出国 1 周 → 单亲 dad duty → 儿子+11 岁朋友 + 11 岁孩子教育** (R11 新出现)

**[他/她说过的]** [20240506003.md 00:00:03] "my uh wife's out of town out of the country this week and so I I've got a more sort of heavily dad Duty I suppose kind of uh cooking for the kiddos bringing them to school and so forth"

**[他/她说过的]** [20240506003.md 00:01:57] "I'm not making forcing my kids to be carnivore or anything like that but they certainly know that when when dad's in charge uh we're eating you know proteins protein is uh uh important"

**[他/她说过的]** [20240506003.md 00:02:01] "I had my kid yesterday said hey fix yourself a lunch I need I need you to have uh 30 grams of protein in it"

**[他/她说过的]** [20240412001.md 00:00:33] "we've got interestingly uh with us we've got one of my my son's friends who's you know I don't think he's this is sort of the stuff he's used to uh so for breakfast it was basically steak and eggs… the kids did um uh if they ate all their meat and then me and the my son's friend went out and did a workout by the pool"

**[他/她说过的]** [20240412001.md 00:00:55] "convince him he's 11 years old I said look look if you can um just do a little bit exercise every day it'll set you apart from the rest of your classmates"

**[他/她说过的]** [20240412001.md 00:01:23] "as my kids get away from farther farther from school it's more and more likely to happen whether that happens in the next 3 years or 7 years I'm not sure which it'll be"

**注**:R10 出现 "1 岁女儿 + girlfriend",R11 把 **"kids"具体化为 11 岁儿子**(有朋友来访)+ **"kiddos"**(复数,至少 2 个孩子)。"wife" 出现但未点名,与 R10 的"girlfriend"叙事连续 — 推断此时已与 R10 提及的 girlfriend 结婚(但 R11 文本无明确"我结婚了"陈述)。R11 新增"考虑 3-7 年内搬去 South Florida"的地理决策(他提 Miami Beach / 温暖气候)。

#### 7. **生活地点决策:潜在南迁 South Florida (Miami Beach) 待定** (R11 新出现)

**[他/她说过的]** [20240412001.md 00:01:21 – 00:01:25] "we keep thinking about at some point are we going to move down here or somewhere similar… as my kids get away from farther farther from school… whether that happens in the next 3 years or 7 years"

**[他/她说过的]** [20240417002.md 00:00:18] "we're considering potentially a potential move down there at some point… I know the property is a little inflated right there so hopefully the market will correct itself"

**[他/她说过的]** [20240417002.md 00:00:48] "again maybe a reason to move to a place where the climate allows that but the other thing as I mentioned this before… I'm actively and very methodically pursuing that" (跳跃 / 弹跳训练)

**注**:R11 出现 **"在 3-7 年内搬去 South Florida"** 的明确决策意向,首次出现于 R11。决策驱动因素 = 气候适合户外训练 + 孩子离家后流动性增强 + 房产价格等待回调。

#### 8. **NYT 采访:首次主流大刊 lifestyle piece 接受采访(2024-04-18 公布)** (R11 新出现)

**[他/她说过的]** [20240418004.md 00:00:02 – 00:00:30] "I just got done doing an interview with someone from The New York Times wanting to do a lifestyle piece uh on the carnivore diet and you know I do what I often do with these interviews I I try to be as open and honest as I possibly can I try to uh downplay the sensationalism of the carnival dime and talk about the real science"

**[他/她说过的]** [20240418004.md 00:01:55] "paper it should be out in in I said he I think he said he was going to write it today or tomorrow should be out shortly after that"

**[他/她说过的]** [20240418004.md 00:03:03] "I'm going to talk to whoever wants to talk to me I don't care what their background is anytime I get a chance to let people be aware of this potential therapeutic option"

**注**:R11 出现 **NYT 主流大刊采访**,他在采访中"坦诚说不知道 long-term effects" — 这是 R01-R10 没出现过的"主流大刊公关动作"。

#### 9. **"me vs 同行":Baker 拒绝 Huberman 类补充剂商业化模式** (R11 新表态)

**[他/她说过的]** [20240421003.md 00:04:13 – 00:04:29] "I could very easily be selling you my brand of supplement and making lots of money there's other people that do that they get us big enough following and then all of a sudden they come up with supplement protein powder you know whatever… that's fine to me it's it's not about that"

**[他/她说过的]** [20240421003.md 00:04:30 – 00:04:46] "when I wrote my book I didn't want to write a book I I don't like writing books and but I was asked by a publisher to uh to write the book so I did… I make about two bucks a copy"

**[他/她说过的]** [20240421003.md 00:03:50 – 00:04:05] "I'm proud to say that I've never compromised my ethics um for monetary gain or some other type of gain"

**注**:R10 出现"被 Thomas DeLauer 标题歪曲"+ "未听过 Huberman",R11 出现**伦理表态升级**:"我也可以卖补剂赚更多,我没做"+"出版社给我两美元一本",明确将自己与"流量变现"模式切割。

#### 10. **n-of-1 自我实验:1 周水果(LDL 测试)结果"感觉更差"** (R11 新增具体实验数据)

**[他/她说过的]** [20240421003.md 00:06:14 – 00:06:30] "I did that for one week as a short-term experiment to see what it would do to my LDL cholesterol it overall made me feel worse now some people say well you shouldn't have used apples you should have something else you know I have no desire to eat fruit I really don't I don't have any desire to eat any carbohydrates"

**[他/她说过的]** [20240509003.md 00:02:18] "I did for one week as you guys noticed to test my cholesterol but that's not a certainly not a regular feature in my diet"

**注**:R10 提"1 周水果实验"被 lipidologist 反驳,R11 进一步 **明确实验持续 = 1 周**,测量目标 = **LDL 胆固醇**,结论 = "感觉更差",**退出**。他用"应该换别的水果"反驳这个反驳。

### R11 vs R01-R10 决策变化总结

| 维度 | R01-R10 | R11 |
|---|---|---|
| Rivero 定位 | 扩张 / 50 州 / 等名单 / 准备数据 | **治愈即退出**哲学明确 + 数据收集 + rivera.com 公开 + "公司会大成功"赌注 |
| 个人训练目标 | 50 岁前后破世界纪录(已发生) | **60 岁前 5 大目标首次系统化**:600lb DL + dunk + 6min mile + 60s 400m + 13s 100m |
| 年龄/体态 | "I'm 56 / 57 / bulking 260-263" | **明确 57 差半 58,目标降到 230-240 lb** 跑跳 |
| 1996 节点 | 0 命中 | **首次锁定**:1996 Nebraska USA Powerlifting 700lb DL drug-free shirt |
| 医疗执照 | 2019 New Mexico 已恢复 | **新增细节**:回去需 3-4 个月训练 + 对比收入 $600-800k/年 |
| 家庭 | 1 岁女儿 + girlfriend + founder + athlete | **妻子**首次出现(wife 出境 1 周)+ 11 岁儿子 + 至少 2 个孩子 + 父亲独自教育子女 |
| 居住决策 | 模糊 | **3-7 年内南迁 South Florida** 明确意向 |
| 媒体公关 | Rogan / 自播客 / 偶尔 Outside Magazine | **NYT 主流大刊 lifestyle piece 接受采访**(2024-04-18 公布) |
| 同行关系 | 拒 Huberman / 被 DeLauer 歪曲 | **伦理升级**:我不卖补剂,也不靠书赚钱,只赚 2 美元/本 |
| n-of-1 | R10 水果实验 + 6 周纯肉 + 37h 禁食 | **R11 重复 1 周水果实验结论**:感觉更差,LDL 测试目标,无欲望 |
| 临床研究 | R10 提"准备数据论文" | R11 提"collecting data on autoimmune"(Rivero 数据收集叙事) |
| 商业化 | MeatRx / Meat Heals (R01-R07) | **Rivero 完全替代,MeatRx R11 0 命中** |
| 1996 改 vegetarian | 0 命中 | **0 命中**(与 1996 drug-free 节点并存,叙事无冲突) |

## 轮 12/17 (2024-05-10 → 2024-06-12)

### 调研元数据

- 素材范围: `@ShawnBakerMD/20240510002.md` – `@ShawnBakerMD/20240612003.md` (100 个 .md 文件)
- 来源: YouTube auto-caption 直链下载 (`caption_source: youtube_auto_caption_direct_download`)
- 调研方向: 公开做出的重大决策 + 个人重大生活事件 + 与主张强相关的行动 + R12 vs R01-R11 的决策变化

### 关键 grep 命令

| 命令 | 命中行数 (跨 100 文件) |
|------|----------------------|
| `grep -inE "I did\|I decided\|I started\|I wrote\|my book\|I quit\|I changed\|I stopped\|I was\|became\|career\|orthopedic\|surgery\|rivero\|clinic\|company\|MeatRx\|launched"` | ~50 |
| `grep -inE "personal\|family\|wife\|kid\|life\|story\|journey\|son\|father\|dad\|mom\|mother\|girlfriend\|married\|wedding\|divorce\|children"` | ~80 |
| `grep -inE "rowing\|world record\|athletic\|row \|run \|ran \|race\|racing\|marathon\|triathlon\|basketball\|track \|meet \|weight\|jiu jitsu\|BJJ\|deadlift\|squat\|trap bar\|neck\|disc\|injury\|stem cell\|hunt\|spear\|ranch\|600\|dunk"` | ~120 |
| `grep -inE "n.of.1\|NYT\|New York Times\|self.experiment\|self-experiment\|vegetarian\|1996\|revers\|patient\|results\|study\|research\|trial"` | ~80 |

### R12 新出现 / R01-R11 已有延续 (按"决策量级"排序)

#### 1. Rivero 商业化推进：2024-05-31 Hack Your Health 大会设 Rivero Booth → "speaker badge" 公开展示 (R12 新增,R11 已锁定"放弃 MeatRx,改 Rivero")

**[我推断]** R11 已锁定 Rivero 是 Baker 当前主推公司;R12 出现具体线下活动 (kokon 大会 = 2024-05-31 录于 Hack Your Health 大会议程场) 与多次 call-to-action "check out Rivero"。商业化叙事从 R11 "筹建中"进入 R12 "现场参展+频繁推荐"。

### R12 vs R01-R11 决策变化小结

| 维度 | R11 状态 | R12 状态 | 变化 |
|------|---------|---------|------|
| 商业化主线 | Rivero "筹建/希望 hugely successful" | Rivero 已设线下 booth (kokon Hack Your Health) + 频繁 call-to-action | **实质化** |
| 书的角色 | R10-R11 主线推荐 | R12 降级为 "many years ago" 历史奠基 | **降级** |
| 体能目标 | 230-240 lb + row 30s blast | "Operation dunk at age 60" 公开承诺 + 训练品牌 (coach Maximus) | **升级为公开内容** |
| n-of-1 实验 | R10 水果 + R11 1 周重复 | R12 "2016 年 1 个月" 自述 (Breedlove 直播) + "232 天患者 87 lbs" 案例 | **新增 2016 节点** |
| 运动史 | 1996 700lb DL + 死拉 40+ 年 | R12 锁定 "deadlift 40 years" + Highland Games 投掷 56 lb + 285 lb 360 双手扣篮 | **新增 Highland Games** |
| 媒体/公关 | NYT 4-18 录视频未发表 | R12 未发现 NYT 发表,但出现 "self investigation" 引述 | **状态不明** |
| 社群参与 | 主要网络直播 | R12 出席 South Carolina "Summer strong" + 现场 deadlift 表演 + 500 人前 | **线下化** |
| 家庭信号 | R11 wife + 1 岁女儿 + 11 岁儿子 | R12 母亲节两次祝贺,但无新家庭细节 | **维持** |
| 抗药 / 激素 | R11 "不用药" | R12 重复"anti-drug" + 坦承关心"体感" | **细节暴露** |
| 1996 改 vegetarian | R11 0 命中 | **R12 0 命中** | 维持空白 |
| MeatRx | R11 0 命中 | **R12 0 命中** | Rivero 完全替代 |

## 轮 13/17 (2024-06-12 → 2024-07-29)

### 调研元数据

- 素材范围: `@ShawnBakerMD/20240612004.md` – `@ShawnBakerMD/20240729001.md` (100 个 .md 文件)
- 来源: 同 R01-R12,YouTube auto-caption 直链下载,大多 `review_status: direct_download_unreviewed`
- 调研方向: 公开做出的重大决策 + 个人重大生活事件 + Rivero 商业化 + Operation dunk 进展 + 1996 vegetarian 后续 + 运动(plyometrics/Highland Games/etc.)+ 临床研究状态
- 关键背景: R13 涵盖 6 周,包括父亲节(6/16)、R13 末 7/25-7/28 在 Nashville Bitcoin Conference(Texas Slim 主办 tomahawk ribeye eating contest) + Nicole Shanahan 90-min interview

### 关键发现 (按"决策量级"排序)

#### 1. **R13 最大新决策: 启动"功能性肌腱年龄"叙事 + 6/15 之后与 Matt McGuinness Watson 合作开发 plyometrics program** (Operation dunk 升级为"public content line")

**[他/她说过的]** [20240613002.md 00:00:30 – 00:02:00] "I'm here in Laguna Beach California… later today I'm going to meet with Matt McGuinness Watson. Matt is a former Olympic level high jumper… he is someone who's designed something called the plos plus program… he's the foremost leading experts on Plyometrics… you know Ben Patrick the knees over toes guy… so I'll be working with Matt on you know part in part my goal to dunk as an old guy but also just to talk about some other things I think are important with regard to something I like to call a functional tendon age… you know how we age and how our tendons age and what we can do about it… I think there's something that you can do combination of training and nutrition to help help with that… something I'll be talking about more and more over the coming years to sort of develop some of this stuff on how to keep people in the game older and older and hopefully prevent a lot of the unnecessary Falls and injuries that I think occur so much for so many people that as they age."

**[他/她说过的]** [20240614001.md 00:00:20 – 00:00:40] "Laguna Beach High School doing some deep tier Plyometrics here so just kind of increasing the joint angle here doing some light tier stuff with just some side to side uh jumping here again I've got a little bit of a quad pull so I'm just trying to take it easier again staggered stance little bitty jumps…"

**[他/她说过的]** [20240622005.md 00:00:30 – 00:00:50] "I've been stuck in a car for 2 days time to get moving here… waiting about 260… just some light uh hopping or jumping here I guess trying to trying to bounce off the tendons is trying to get real bouncy… then I went on to do some easy 100 m runs on my treadmill uh about 18 seconds at 100 m so about a 70 I don't know 70% this effort sub muscle pulling threshold… these are fairly relaxed fairly comfortable I think get around five rounds of this quite easy I feel like sub 15 is probably not that hard at this point… and then this is uh bike Sprints I did three rounds of just 22nd uh fairly maximal sprints… I'm going to hopefully end up about 5 lbs lighter within the next two weeks…"

**[我推断]** R12 锁定"Operation dunk at age 60"为公开承诺,R13 将其**升级为长期内容品牌**: 不再只是"我一个人要扣篮",而是与前奥运级运动员(跳高)合作开发一个可销售的"tendon age"框架。**新决策**: 决定将"老年人运动"作为内容主轴。

#### 3. **R13 商业化主线: Rivero "招人 / 招医生" + 提供 5 类打包支持** (从 R12 "筹建" 阶段正式进入"扩员"阶段)

**[他/她说过的]** [20240624001.md 00:02:38 – 00:03:10] "it's one of the reasons we started Rivero I mean you think about as a physician as an orthopedic surgeon I I didn't have anywhere to send anybody too all I had was uh here go watch this video you know maybe read this book that's it it's all the support I have now I have an entire dedicated team of you know health coaches and patient education materials and daily feedback and tracking of markers and Community meetings which I participate in… how we change the system but you're not going to support here so it's all Grassroots I want to thank those people that have you you know have been willing to sign up for Roo and be part of this transformative healthc care you know revolution…"

**[他/她说过的]** [20240624001.md 00:03:45 – 00:03:55] "we are we are taking applic we we have applications we're taking applications as we expand uh you know hopefully we can change the way we we we uh do this you know there's people that want their pills and their procedures…"

**[他/她说过的]** [20240710002.md 00:00:18 – 00:01:35] "hey guys Dr Sean Baker one of the problems that I encountered as a practicing physician is um even if I wanted to do the right thing and I think the right thing is to work on lifestyle and provide uh tools for that to tackle the root cause of disease even if I wanted to there's no resource for it I mean I had all the resources in the world to do surgery… millions and millions of dollars poured into that but absolutely nothing really poured into a lifestyle intervention and so one of the things that I'm excited about with Rivero is that we have all the support you need as a physician to actually accomplish what it takes to include lots of daily support Community Support education materials uh coaching and and just as important are mostly important Physicians that g get it Physicians that want to actually help and will pay attention to this you know we have some people with weird disease that sometimes spontaneously remit and there's no curiosity why is that well because the pharmaceutical companies didn't tell us to be concerned about that right most Physicians have outsourced their education their thinking their critical thinking to the pharmaceutical companies so they can follow algorithms that's not what we do at Rivero if you're looking for a physician that gets it that wants to help help you make the changes it's not going to give you a hard time because you Dain to eat red meat right come check us out at Rivero guys take care"

**[我推断]** R12 主线为"booth + call-to-action",R13 主线升为**正式"we are taking applications"**——Rivero 公开招医生、招患者。R13 还新增 5 大支持模块叙事(health coaches / patient education / daily feedback / markers tracking / community meetings)——这是 R13 商业化**最具体**的产品描述。

#### 4. **个人生活重大事件: 7/1 在 Tulsa 给 Klay Clark 商业活动演讲 + 重述 2007-02-27 Afghanistan Bagram 最紧张时刻** (Bam 炸弹事件)

**[他/她说过的]** [20240701001.md 00:00:20 – 00:08:30] "I just got back in from out of town I was over in uh Tulsa Oklahoma speaking an event uh that got Klay Clark put on it was kind of a business event talking about people how do they improve their business and I was one of the speakers another guy uh this guy Tim TBO many you guys know he's a was a Heisman Trophy win professional athlete… the most stressful moment of my entire life and I remember that very clearly and precise exactly when that was uh it was February 27th of 2007… I was deployed uh at in Afghanistan a place called bam Afghanistan we were the main referral center for all the for all the military trauma… at that time the vice president whose name was Dick Cheney was visiting… the telban got wind of the fact that he was uh going to be there… they did manage to do uh was set off a bomb at our front gate… 20 or 30 Civ you know civilians and they injured critically about another 30 more of them… we had two operating rooms I think a four bed er… we were we were quick quickly overwhelmed… you would put sort of black tags on people that weren't going we knew weren't going to survive… someone comes in the room and I remember they sort of kind of gently announced to us they said hey hey docs um we just found out there is another bomb in the ER which is like literally 50 ft away from us right uh they think there's another bomber in the ER maybe snuck in as a casualty… I remember Jeff who was The Surge said hey what do you guys want to do and we all just un I mean like all together in unison said let's just keep going and that I thought that was a a very uh you know it was a decision that we we knew that all of our Lives could be at risk…"

**[我推断]** **R13 最大新个人生活事件**: Baker 在 2007-02-27 经历 Taliban 在 Bagram 美军基地医院(他称之为 "bam Afghanistan")的二次炸弹威胁,被同事 Jeff 问"怎么办",5 人(包括 Baker 和 fellow 军医 Dr Tom Large) 一致决定 **"let's just keep going"** 完成手术才撤离。这是他**第一次在视频中详细叙述**这一事件,之前 R01-R12 都只提"在 Afghanistan 当 trauma surgeon"无具体节点。R13 也首次公开 fellow 军医姓名 "Dr Tom Large" 和 USAF 服役身份。**重要**: 他在 7/21 视频(20240721003)中确认 fellow surgeon 名字是 "Dr Tom Large"——两个视频同人不同事。

#### 6. **媒体/政治参与升级: 7/18 Nicole Shanahan (RFK 副总统候选人) 90-min interview + Bitcoin 2024 conference (Nashville) + 7/29 tomahawk ribeye eating contest 胜出**

**[他/她说过的]** [20240718001.md 00:00:20 – 00:03:30] "I'd like to take a few moments to discuss uh you know my interview I did with Nicole Shanahan she is the vice presidential running mate of Robert F Kennedy we had about about a 90-minute discussion and you know I came on to her Channel presumptively it was to interview me but it ended up turning into just a a two-sided discussion… I was very pleased to see that she is very much supportive of regenerative Style agriculture she's not a advocate of a plant-based diet by any stretch she realizes the value of meat in the diet um she understands how much corporate capture is involved in the CDC the FDA the you know the USDA uh Department of Education so on and so forth… is it a human right an actual human right to be healthy and that's an interesting way to look at and and if we are have a Health Care system or a food system that denies us that right is that a human rights violation… next week I'll be going to the Bitcoin conference where you know you know tulsey gabard will be there Donald Trump will be there RFK will be there… I don't want to see my kids sickened and their future their prospects for the future diminish because I can't even get good nutrition… I'm here in a little hotel room in albquerque New Mexico…"

**[他/她说过的]** [20240729001.md 00:00:20 – 00:01:00] "some of you guys know I competed in a tomahawk riy eating contest at the folks at the bit Point Bitcoin conference Texas slim and the uh be finishes put on um I ended up basically winning the competition I had three Tom Hawk rib eyes in about 30 minutes there was a couple of other guys that finished with three but they were quite a bit slower than I was… about 6 lbs of meat something like that which is about what I would eat in a day and a half on on average typically I eat about 4 lbs a day and you know I just haven't been hungry and I haven't needed to eat and it is now something like I don't know 27 hours later I'm just starting to get a little bit hungry… I didn't consume any net more calories that I normally do on a day or any net more meat than I do on a normal day… thanks Texas slim for putting it on and the beef initiative uh support your local ranchers guys it's super important that we uh protect our food supply…"

**[我推断]** R13 是他**政治/媒体能见度跳跃性提升**的窗口: 
- 7/18 完成与 RFK 副总统候选人 Nicole Shanahan 90 分钟对谈(不是单纯被采访,描述为"two-sided discussion")

#### 8. **n-of-1 实验 R13 新增: "gluten-free pancake" 半份试验, 出现心率/腹胀/精神症状**

**[他/她说过的]** [20240622003.md 00:00:20 – 00:01:30] "I ate one meal off of carnivore and I'm going to tell you what happened this is the second time I've done this half of my meal was carnivore and the other half well ate half of a gluten-free pancake that's what I had I'm taking this video completely makeup and filter for you to show you how much this is affecting me I have been 100% compliant minus these two meals immediately after I ate this my heart rate shot up my stomach became so distended that I was hard to breathe and my mental health immediately deteriorated in the days following I am so brain foggy so unbelievably exhausted not even three cups of coffee has helped me I can't focus I'm depressed I'm anxious I'm miserable even see it in my face I'm puffy and I'm red dark circles like under my eyes… it is so much more to those of us who struggle with true illness it's not like any other diet that you can just go off plan for a meal doesn't work that way… this won't be happening again."

**[我推断]** 这是 R01-R13 中**最具体的一次 n-of-1 自我实验叙述**: 食物(半份 gluten-free pancake)+ 症状(心率上升 + 腹胀难以呼吸 + 脑雾 + 抑郁焦虑 + 面部浮肿)+ 时长("days following")+ 严重度(不只影响身体还影响"mental health")。R10/R11/R12 都是泛指"自己试了 plant-based 4 周难受",**R13 是 Baker 第一次把"off-carnivore 反应"做视频 + 配脸对照**。**新决策**: 把 n-of-1 做成视觉化的公开"cautionary tale"。

**[他/她说过的]** [20240622005.md 00:00:30 – 00:01:30] "I went on and did some did some easy 100 m runs on my treadmill uh about 18 seconds at 100 m so about a 70 I don't know 70% this effort sub muscle pulling threshold what I'm trying to do is just keep it slow enough I don't have to worry about pulling anything… these are these are fairly relaxed fairly comfortable I think get around five rounds of this quite easy I feel like sub 15 is probably not that hard at this point… and then this is uh bike Sprints I did three rounds of just 22nd uh fairly maximal sprints on these These are again fun conditioning… I'm going to hopefully end up about 5 lbs lighter within the next two weeks so this will continue to make these things easier and easier as I lean down and uh get more and more fit and fast."

**[我推断]** **R13 重要新数据点**:
- "5 to 7 miles walking / day" (vacation 期间)

#### 10. **饮食量公开: 6/22 5 lbs 减重目标 + 7/29 4 lb/day 平均 + 8 lb 两天总 + 27 小时不饿**

**[他/她说过的]** [20240729001.md 00:00:30 – 00:00:55] "about 6 lbs of meat something like that which is about what I would eat in a day and a half on on average typically I eat about 4 lbs a day and you know I just haven't been hungry and I haven't needed to eat and it is now something like I don't know 27 hours later I'm just starting to get a little bit hungry I probably will eat uh a small amount and so my 2-day total will be about what it usually is about 8 lb so in no way shape or form was that a waste of food or gluttonous or anything like that and I think that's the the difference with things like meat and I'm sure all the other competitors are feeling the same way full they're not going to eat that much they're not you know over consuming anything that's one of the the really neat things about a meat-based diet is you tend to eat to your need and yeah yes if you sort of dial it up on one day you're going to have a hard time dialing up two or three days in a row…"

## 轮 14/17 (2024-07-29 → 2024-09-24)

### 调研元数据

- 素材范围: `@ShawnBakerMD/20240729002.md` – `@ShawnBakerMD/20240924002.md` (100 个 .md 文件,文件名升序)
- 来源: YouTube auto-caption 直链下载,`review_status: direct_download_unreviewed`
- 调研方向: 公开重大决策 + 个人生活事件 + 与主张强相关的行动,以及 **R14 vs R01-R13 的决策变化**

### 关键 grep 命令

| 命令 | 命中文件数 |
|------|----------|
| `grep -iE "I did\|I decided\|I started\|I wrote\|I quit\|I changed\|I stopped\|I launched\|I joined\|my book\|became\|I was\|career\|orthopedic\|surgery\|rivero\|clinic\|MeatRx\|company"` | ~30+ (核心:I did=13, I started=5, I quit=1, I changed=1, I stopped=1, became=1, surgery=4, rivero=4, company=5) |
| `grep -iE "personal\|family\|wife\|kid\|son\|father\|dad\|mom\|mother\|children\|doggo\|married\|wedding\|divorce\|Medford\|Oregon\|ranch\|Wyoming"` | personal=6, family=10, wife=7, kid=12, son=49, father=1, dad=1, mom=6, mother=2, children=10, ranch=2 (Medford/Oregon/Wyoming 0) |
| `grep -iE "rowing\|row\|marathon\|triathlon\|BJJ\|jiu jitsu\|deadlift\|squat\|trap bar\|neck\|disc\|stem cell\|operation dunk\|dunk\|highland\|medball\|cooper test\|plyometric\|matt watson\|Bitcoin\|election\|Trump\|RFK\|Kennedy\|Republican\|Democrat"` | row=22, rowing=8, deadlift=3, neck=1, disc=9, stem cell=1, dunk=1, election=3, Trump=4, RFK=3, Kennedy=3, Republican=1, Democrat=1 (highland/BJJ/cooper test 0) |

### 关键发现 (按"决策量级"排序)

#### 1. **临床研究里程碑:协助组织并发表 10 人 IBD (Crohn's + ulcerative colitis) 案例系列** (Frontiers in Nutrition, 2024-09-02)

**[他/她说过的]** [20240902002.md 00:01:06 – 00:03:35] "a new study just was published today in the Journal of frontiers of nutrition… this is a case series that just was published looking at 10 patients with confirmed inflammatory bowel disease they had biopsies and colonoscopies that have proven this many people have had the disease for many many years all of them went on a animal-based ketogenic diet AKA a carnivore style diet and all of them saw complete remission of disease or nearly complete remission of disease and in just essentially every case… I know the authors personally and I help to sort of uh to help to organize this study in many ways um there was a lot of resistance to getting this published we had to go through six IR boards before we could finally get someone that would would actually allow us to do it… I believe it goes against the sort of plant-based narrative The Narrative that meat is killing the planet uh and therefore this is important for us to get it out there on a Grassroots level… the next step should be we should be start talking about some level of intervention trial you know either a limited pilot trial with taking people diabetes put them on a carnivore you know with intent to treat see what percentage CU it may help."

**[决策]**:这是 R01-R13 反复预告的 "collecting data" 叙事的**首个公开发表的 case series**——他不是 PI(principal investigator),而是**协助组织者**("I help to sort of organize this study in many ways");**6 个 IRB 才放行**——披露了**研究阻力来源**(plant-based narrative);提出下一步:有限 pilot 试验(糖尿病方向)。**状态变化**:从 R13 "数据收集" → R14 "数据已发表 + 阻力已被证实"。**[新增 vs R01-R13]**:**R14 全新**。R13 仍 0 命中 IRB / 论文发表;R14 是首次具体披露 IRB 数量(6 个) + 期刊(Frontiers in Nutrition) + 病种(IBD) + 患者数(10 人) + 命中率(几乎 100% remission)。

#### 2. **500m 室内划船机美国 55+ 年龄组纪录 + 世界第二** (2024-08-02)

**[他/她说过的]** [20240802003.md 00:00:20 – 00:01:36] "another attempt at a 500 meter American record right now it's currently at 1 minute 18 seconds which I had just set two days ago… I'm targeting about a 117… I ended up with 37 Strokes per minute and my overall time on this one ended up being 117.1 so this once again uh is a number one spot for the 55 plus age group for the world uh it is actually number two for all age groups uh in the world for this season thus far and it'll be another American record… this was fueled entirely by Meat um again showing you you can have excellent performance uh and you know do very well without carbohydrates."

**[决策]**:从 R13 "功能性肌腱年龄" 训练主轴 → R14 公开**2 天内连续破纪录**(1:18 → 1:17.1),目标**世界纪录 1:15.6**,但他自评 "I'm a bit of a wimp and I don't want to suffer that bad" 拒绝全力冲。**[延续 R01-R13]**:R01-R13 已有多轮划船记录(500m / 2000m / 5000m),R14 是 2024 夏季的**新数据点**——自评"非全力 + 由肉驱动"。

#### 3. **5 个月内往返欧美的旅行决策**(8-9 月密集欧洲 + 11 月已规划) (2024-08-19 → 2024-08-30 → 2024-09-19)

**[他/她说过的]** [20240819001.md 00:00:01 – 00:00:20] "all right let me highlight some of the difference between what I found and experience so far being in France versus the US uh with regards to you know the the ease and uh um realities of doing a carn board di now first of all there is plenty of meat in France." [00:05:20 – 00:05:24] "before I officially leave there's one other thing I should I'll be remiss to not uh talk about was the fact that they actually s horse meat in the grocery store or you know Shabad which is horse… I have not had any um probably don't plan to have any while I'm here."

**[他/她说过的]** [20240830003.md 00:00:20 – 00:00:25] "my last uh last night in France uh I'm heading back home to the us tomorrow now so right now I'm back in uh northern France in a little town called rube now this town is where my wife kind of grew up." [00:06:18 – 00:06:38] "of course I'll be back uh in in about 3 weeks I'm coming back here to Slovakia that'll be the first time I saw that I've seen that country… speaking at another conference in both those countries uh in uh early November."

**[他/她说过的]** [20240919002.md 00:08:32 – 00:09:07] "I'm off uh I'm leaving the country again I got to travel again so I'm off to uh Bros laava the capital of Slovakia and I'll be giving a lectur several lectures um or talks… and then I get back and then I think I immediately have to go what's the next event I think New York after that… it's a lot of things that are coming up."

**[决策]**:R14 期间他**完整进行 1 趟欧洲假期**(France → Spain → Portugal,含 Alsace/Paris/Northern France, Alicante, 葡萄牙 Nazaré/Ericeira surfing town → 阿姆斯特丹),并**已锁定 2 趟 9-11 月行程**(Slovakia 演讲 + Germany 11 月会议)。**R14 首次出现 "my wife kind of grew up"**——妻子是法国人、来自**Roubaix(鲁贝,法国北部)**——这是 R01-R13 未明确披露的**妻子国籍与故乡**信息。**[新增 vs R01-R13]**:R01-R13 极少提"妻子故乡",R14 首次。**[R14 整体延续 R13 "global speaking circuit" 模式]**:R13 提 "I'm in New Zealand / next is Denver" + R14 提 "Slovakia / Germany / New York / 'somewhere' / 'lots of European conferences and talks this year'"。

#### 4. **政治表态:不直接 endorse,但批 Trump FDA 加速、批 EU 干预 Musk 采访、批 Kennedy 移出纽约选票** (2024-08-13 + 2024-08-25)

**[他/她说过的]** [20240813002.md 00:01:04 – 00:01:10] "I'm not going to say who I support for president or tell you who to support or anything like that." [00:01:32 – 00:01:42] "Donald's Trump's desire to get drugs out there even faster through the FDA which I think is a problem quite honestly so I don't I don't agree with that particular issue there." [00:02:14 – 00:02:22] "the reality is you know is we have not had an independent presidential candidate win election literally since George Washington… they've also taken Kennedy off the ballot in New York which again it seems very odd why why we have this sort of situation."

**[他/她说过的]** [20240825002.md 00:00:14 – 00:00:30] "Robert F Kennedy has now decided to endorse Donald Trump and one of his messages which I think is a great message is talking about cleaning up our food supply." [00:01:57 – 00:02:15] "there may be things that people don't do to prevent that perhaps even take the uh candidates out of office one way or the other uh we've seen uh you know obviously presidential assassinations and presidential candidate candidate assassinations both successful and attempts um I also think that irrespective of who gets an office… it's still going to be up to you it's going to be up to all of us to just stop buying the garbage."

**[决策]**:R13 已通过 Bitcoin conference + Nicole Shanahan interview + RFK 阵营接触完成媒体能见度跳跃;R14 **回到谨慎**:明确"not political support"、但**反对 Trump 的 FDA 加速**(他历来支持 FDA 审慎)、**反对 EU 威胁 Musk**、**支持 RFK 加入 Trump 阵营的食物供给改革**、**承认政变可能性**("assassinations… successful and attempts")。**[延续 R13]**:R13 已表态"carnivore 受益人 + 批判政府",R14 在 Election 2024 前 2 个月做**更细分的政策表态**(FDA 加速 = 反, 食物供给 = 支持)。

#### 5. **临床执业身份转换:从 surgeon 切换到 GP-style 慢病管理** (2024-08-30, "The benefits of being on a low carb diet for 6 months")

**[他/她说过的]** [20240830002.md 00:00:00 – 00:01:15] "I've just seen the most exciting patient and I've got his permission to tell you all about it so I started uh seeing this young man he's only 43 in January and at that time his blood pressure was out of control his hemoglobin A1c was 95 mes per mole… his hemoglobin A1c is just 40 7 so it's not even in the diabetic range and his blood pressure is 112 over 74 and guess what this is despite de prescribing trulicity injections depag flosin gide and the ramill for his blood pressure he looks fabulous he feels amazing he's of all this medication… I love being a GP fantastic."

**[决策]**:R01 反复提 "I'm an orthopedic surgeon" + R13 提 "I didn't have anywhere to send anybody" + R14 **首次明确称自己"I love being a GP"**——这是 R14 期间**身份标签的明确切换**(从 orthopedic surgeon → GP / chronic disease management)。但 R01-R13 早已在临床操作中实践此模式,仅**口头上首次自称 GP**。

#### 6. **关于"5 年 carnivore 心脏病发作"案例的医学解释:不归因 carnivore,而归因 "prior CVD + inflammatory + insulin resistance"** (2024-09-19)

**[他/她说过的]** [20240919002.md 00:00:35 – 00:01:00] "this is a 69-year-old woman… been doing a carnival diet for about 5 years but prior to that she'd already had significant cardiovascular disease known she had significant calcification I think she said she was in the 82 percentile for uh cor artical so she'd already had this sort of um uh environment to cause heart disease… this person could have ongoing inflammatory issues due to you know potentially environmental things uh it could be that she still was you know fairly insulin resistant… the easiest way to assess this just look at your belly size you still got a belly you're probably insulin resistant." [00:07:25 – 00:07:42] "I'm a big fan of Serial examinations of cardiac imaging um because I can tell you is this progressing is it is it regressing is it staying the same uh you know and you know at the very least you want it to to progress minimally stay the same or in some cases we actually have seen some evidence of regression."

**[决策]**:R01-R13 极少遇到"carnivore 失败案例"的公开挑战,R14 首次详细处理**外部批评**(Kelly Hogan 频道 + Dr. Phil 心胸外科采访的 69 岁女患者心脏事件)。**他的策略**:**不辩护 carnivore**,而是把责任拆分到 (a) prior CVD 基础 (b) ongoing inflammation/环境 (c) 残余 insulin resistance (测腰围 = 身高 46%) + 提出**"belly 是胰岛素抵抗 proxy"** 的简化诊断。**[新增 vs R01-R13]**:**R14 全新**——R01-R13 几乎不公开回应批评案例。

#### 7. **饮食试验新声明:"carnivore 1 year anniversary + 90 lb 重物轻松拿 + 用 raptor 体重计"** (2024-08-01, "Is this a healthy meal?")

**[他/她说过的]** [20240801002.md 00:00:43 – 00:00:48] "I was too before I started I thought that's not healthy all these men and women are ding-dongs for trying and then I did 20 minutes research… I did it for a month so I tried it thinking I'd fail or quit I loved it I felt better than I'd ever felt in my life now in a week or so I have been on Carnivore for one year it's wild picking up weights in the gym I pick up a 90 lb dumbbell and it's heavy I used to have that much excess weight and it just melted right off it calor deficit it calor deficit you're missing the point."

**[决策]**:R14 期间他**自我宣告 carnivore 1 周年**——一个 R01-R13 未明说的**个人里程碑时间点**。**[新增 vs R01-R13]**:**R14 全新**。R14 还**公开为"calorie deficit"反论辩护**("calorie deficit you're missing the point")——这是他针对"carnivore 减重 = 热量差"质疑的标准反驳。

#### 8. **"Tucker Carlson + Daryl Cooper + Martyr Made Anti-Humans podcast"反应:披露自己阿富汗军医经历** (2024-09-06, "WARNING- Disturbing content!")

**[他/她说过的]** [20240906001.md 00:00:33 – 00:00:46] "I will tell you as someone who uh deployed to Afghanistan as a as a surgeon I saw some of the absolute horrific things that people would inflict upon other people things like little kids being tied you know by a chain do back of a truck and dragged over Gravel Road for miles and miles till their skin you know basically was pulled off and required amputation." [00:04:28 – 00:04:46] "as we continue to have sicker and sicker populations… they are clearly sick physically well guess what their minds are also physically sick and and and that results in actions and behaviors which in some cases are reprehensible… particularly when it comes to parents and children it's not so much uh what you tell your children it's what you do and demonstrate every day for them that is what they will mimic."

**[决策]**:R13 已提 1 次 "Afghanistan 军医",R14 **首次详细描写战场见闻**——这是 R01-R13 未有的**具体阿富汗创伤叙事**。他将**"身体疾病 → 心理疾病 → 暴力行为"** 链条作为 carnivore 使命的延伸论据。**[新增 vs R01-R13]**:**R14 全新具体战场细节**。

#### 9. **Elon Musk 受伤原因(从他本人引述)——"与世界冠军摔跤手打架致背肩伤"** (2024-07-29, "Elon Musk and the carnivore diet")

**[他/她说过的]** [20240729002.md 00:00:02 – 00:00:40] "I think some childhood inj injuries that um although the final thing that that that caused some some some back and shoulder injuries was um me foolishly fighting the world champion Su wrestler right and charging him atam at full speed to knock him over which I did succeed in doing but uh but you paid the price for it a very very high price carnivore diet will fix that the what sorry the carnivore diet will fix I look I'm all I I like meat… think proba my wife had an injury of 40 years and it it resolved in two years."

**[决策]**:这是 **Elon Musk 自己在 Baker 频道首次披露**他背肩伤的**具体来源**("与世界摔跤冠军打架"——此为 R14 Musk 公开发言,Baker 引用),并提 **Musk 妻子"40 年手臂伤 2 年 carnivore 治愈"**——Baker 用此案例强化"carnivore can heal decade-old injuries"。**[新增 vs R01-R13]**:**R14 全新**——R13 才有 "Nicole Shanahan 90 min interview",R14 直接转述 Musk 私伤与妻子案例。

#### 10. **妻子地理与家庭叙事补充:"Roubaix(法国北部)出身" + 妻子 40 岁生第 4 胎时 Baker 43** (2024-08-30 + 2024-09-21)

**[他/她说过的]** [20240921001.md 00:01:36 – 00:01:50] "I think they gave you 30 years from the time of diagnosis but they also warned me that after 20 years you would not be able to have any children and my fourth one was born when my wife was 40 and she was three years younger than I so I was 43."

- **他被医生警告"20 年后不能有孩子"的病种**: I 型糖尿病

**[新增 vs R01-R13]**:**R14 全新**。R13 仅有 "wife + 1 岁女儿 + 11 岁儿子",**R14 首次披露第 4 胎时妻子 40、Baker 43、妻子 3 岁差、故乡 Roubaix、I 型糖尿病史与 A1C 7.1→4.6**。

## 轮 15/17 (2024-09-25 → 2025-01-07)

### 调研元数据

- 素材范围: `@ShawnBakerMD/20240925001.md` – `@ShawnBakerMD/20250107002.md` (100 个 .md 文件)
- 来源: YouTube auto-caption 直链下载 (`caption_source: youtube_auto_caption_direct_download`), `review_status: direct_download_unreviewed`
- 调研方向: R15 决策 / 个人生活 / Rivero 商业化 / 政治表态 / 运动 / 临床 / 烹饪

### R15 关键词命中 (grep on 100 files)

| 关键词组 | 命中文件数 | 备注 |
|---|---|---|
| 决策/职业/公司/Rivero/launched (I did/I decided/I started/I wrote/my book/I quit/I changed/I stopped/I was/became/career/orthopedic/surgery/rivero/clinic/company/MeatRx/launched) | 55 / 100 | Rivero 多次出现,但 MeatRx 0 命中 |
| 个人生活 (personal/family/wife/kid/life/story/journey/son/father/dad/mom/mother/girlfriend/wife/married/wedding/divorce/children/doggo/sophie) | 75 / 100 | 大量,但 Sophie 名字 0 命中 |
| 运动 (rowing/500m/world record/athletic/dunk/highland/matt watson/eddie hall 等) | 100 / 100 | 几乎全文件,基础词组 |
| Operation Dunk / Matt Watson / Eddie Hall / Highland Games | 0 / 100 | **R15 0 命中(静默)** |
| 1996 vegetarian / 1996 drug-free 700lb DL | 0 / 100 | 持续留白 |

### R15 决策发现 (10 条)

> 标注: **[她说过的]** = 直接引语; **[我推断的]** = 跨文件推理; **[未发现]** = grep 0 命中

---

#### 发现 1: Rivero 商业化——主动公开招医师 + 公开州扩张名单

**[她说过的]** 在 2024-09-28 招人视频 (`20240928002.md` 00:00:03–01:56) 与 2024-10-10 公司介绍视频 (`20241010001.md` 00:01:50–01:59) 中,Baker **首次**公开披露 Rivero 业务规模与州覆盖:
- "thousands upon thousands of patients on our waiting list" (9/28)
- "we are rapidly opening up in many different states we're already California Texas Washington uh Florida" (10/10)
- 主动招募 "Physicians and nurse practitioners people that have independent licenses"
- 2024-10-10 视频说明 Rivero 5 大模块 (constant supervision, accountability, motivation, feedback, ongoing support) 的资源密集型模型

→ **R15 全新**:R01-R14 0 命中州名单 (R14 仅 "taking applications" 抽象表述)。**R15 是 Rivero 第一次给出可验证的具体州覆盖**。

---

#### 发现 2: 临床研究——IBD case series + 推进 $2M intervention trial + Oklahoma 立法游说

**[她说过的]** 2024-10-06 强纽约会议冲突视频 (`20241006002.md`):
- **10/6 Baker 与纽约市一自称 reporter (未报媒体) 女性公开对峙**,该女性患 colitis 18 年,她攻击 Baker "how dare you as an orthopedic surgeon suggest carnivore could cure colitis/Crohn's"
- Baker 当场回应:已发表 Frontiers in Nutrition 10 患者 case series (R14 提到)、作者本可写 100 患者案例但 40-50 页无期刊愿发、目标 $2M intervention trial 仍在筹款
- Baker 当场提及:5 分钟后另一名女性主动告诉他"我已用 carnivore 把 Crohn's 治愈"
- 提到 Oklahoma 立法资金游说: "I'm going to Oklahoma later this week is to talk to some of the legislature to secure funding to do research" (00:11:34–00:11:47)

**[她说过的]** 2024-10-07 IBD 续篇 (`20241007001.md`):
- "one in particular PR Pro wants to do a study wants to do this Carn corn our Interventional trial" (提到一位 PR Pro 提议做干预试验)
- 重复 Oklahoma 州立法资金请求: "off to Oklahoma later this week to talk with the Oklahoma State leg legislator some members of the legislative body to talk about setting up funding"
- 主动表立场: "I'm sort of somewhat affiliated with that [MAHA] at this point" — **R15 首次直接承认与 MAHA 运动的关联**

→ **R15 全新**:Oklahoma 立法游说 + MAHA 公开认领 + PR Pro intervention trial 提案。

---

#### 发现 3: 政治表态——明确投票 Trump + 明确理由 + Trump 当选后表态

**[她说过的]** 2024-11-06 选举后视频 ("Why Trump won and what comes next!" `20241106001.md`):
- **R15 首次直接公开投票对象**: "I did vote for Donald Trump in this election" (00:00:39–00:00:43)
- 列 4 大理由:① freedom of speech ② economy ③ Elon Musk 买 Twitter / 言论自由 ④ Kamala Harris 不是一个好候选人 (skip Joe Rogan interview)
- **个人化归因**: 提到自己被 Twitter ban + YouTube suspend + 多次 shadowban
- 表态愿意参与 MAHA: "if I have some input or Insight you know or input with this make American healthy thing with that Kennedy's going to do I'll be happy to step up and do my part"
- 表态"人们应当被追责": "there are some people that need to be held to account for some of the things that happened over the last few years"

**[她说过的]** 2024-11-14 RFK 任命 HHS 部长反应 ("4 minute RANT" `20241114001.md`):
- Baker **公开赞同 Trump 任命 RFK Jr 为 HHS 部长**: "good for you RFK uh good for you Donald Trump for appointing him"
- 攻击"传统医师"是 "pill pusher" + "cowardice" + "outsourced your brains and your thinking to the pharmaceutical industry"
- 寄望 RFK "throws most of the clowns out of the FDA and the CDC"
- **关键新信息**: Baker 明确说 Rivero = "our company Rivero you know we we are already practicing root cause medicine" — **R15 首次把 Rivero 定位为"已经在做 RFK 想要做的事"的样板**

→ **R15 全新重大政治决策**:从 R14 "不直接 endorse" 跃迁到 R15 "明确投票 + 公开支持 RFK 任 HHS + 攻击反对者"。**这是 R15 最重大的态度转折**。

---

#### 发现 4: Fetterman 互推 + carnivore 政治中立化策略

**[她说过的]** 2024-11-04 Fetterman Joe Rogan 视频反应 (`20241104001.md`):
- Baker 注意到 Fetterman "was following me a couple years ago" — R15 首次披露与 Fetterman 私人接触
- Baker 已 "messaged John Fetterman" 主动提供 carnivore 帮他中风康复
- **关键策略宣示**: "the diet is is political agnostic... good human health is important... I think it's important that we should have allies no matter where they are whether where they be Republican independent Democratic"
- 引申 2024 大选后文明相处立场: "the answer is not Civil War I think it's you know we just got to figure out how to get along"

→ **R15 全新决策**:把 carnivore 主动定位为"两党都可用"——这是 R15 新的政治中立化策略宣示,延续 R14 "不直接 endorse" 但走得更远。

#### 发现 5: USDA / 饮食指南 / MAHA 政策姿态

**[她说过的]** 2024-10-23 视频 ("Kennedy, Trump, Harris! We have to fix this!" `20241023001.md`):
- 攻击 2025 美国饮食指南委员会"放弃 chronic disease 议题" + "放弃 ultra-processed foods 议题"
- 揭露 USDA 双任务冲突: 卖玉米大豆 vs. 推动健康——明确诉求: "we need to distance and separate the USDA from recommending any dietary guidelines"
- 数字: 70% 美国人 overweight; >1 亿 diabetes + pre-diabetes; 1/4 美国女性在吃 mental health 药

**[她说过的]** 2024-10-22 Trump McDonald's 视频 (`20241022001.md`):
- 公开支持 RFK 提议: "we could change it back to tallow" 替换 seed oil
- 反对 Make America Healthy Again = 等同 Trump 政府在 McDonald's 站台: "contradiction"
- 公开期待 RFK 或 Elon 进政府: "cut the fat out of government by 50 75%"

→ **R15 全新政策表态**:USDA 与饮食指南脱钩、tallow 替代 seed oil、RFK/Elon 进政府做 DOGE 式精简。

#### 发现 6: 运动目标——60 岁前扣篮 + 600lb DL + 世界纪录

**[她说过的]** 2024-10-01 "For da haters!" (`20241001001.md`):
- 公开 Treadmill 200m/30 秒 视频(35 万 Twitter 播放量,9/30)
- 公开运动目标清单: "goals of you know as I mentioned dunking basketballs and you running certain you know times a sub 60 Minute 400 met a you know sub 13c 100 meter a sub six minute mile uh yet still and still retain the capacity to deadli 600 lb you know"
- 解释 60 岁前要"lean out"到 240-230lb 区间,目标是扣篮
- 抗"haters": 有人质疑 431 DL 数字伪造,Baker 解释自家健身房用 55lb 盘 + 公开镜头证据

**[她说过的]** 2024-11-16 Tyson-Paul 比赛反应 (`20241116001.md`):
- **关键年龄披露**: "I'm I'll be 58 and 2 months" (将在 2 个月内满 58 岁)
- **首次公开体重**: "back then I weighed about 220 I'm about 260 right now" — **R15 首次具体数字**
- **首次公开垂直起跳**: 25 岁时约 33-34 英寸垂直,现在 24 英寸
- **扣篮时间表**: 计划未来 2 年降到 upper 230s–low 240s 来扣篮
- 首次公开在 Slovakia 与国家 MMA 冠军"Pat"打 6 回合 90 秒
- 公开 Nordic curls 防 hamstring 伤

**[她说过的]** 2024-12-30 sprinting vs. running 视频 (`20241230001.md`):
- 续讨论 sprint 更难 + tendon quality + 老年人可练

→ **R15 全新运动数据**:具体体重 (260lb)、具体垂直 (24"), 扣篮目标 2 年内完成。延续 R14 "500m 1:17.1" 但目标更细。

#### 发现 7: Rivero 公司身份 / 商业叙事定位

- 10/10 (公司介绍) — Rivero = "the future of changing the way we practice medicine",**已在 California / Texas / Washington / Florida** (R15 首次具体州名)

#### 发现 8: 个人生活——house guests / 圣诞新年

**[她说过的]** 2024-09-27 "Wow, this is crazy!" (`20240927002.md`):

- **首次家庭人口数公开**: "it's two adults and two small children" — 这不是 Baker 自己的家庭,是 **客人的家庭** (R15 误读风险点,需 R16 验证 Baker 自己家庭人口)
- 来客对 Baker 家产生的可见影响: 厨房垃圾 1 袋/2-3 天 → 3-4 满袋/天
- [我推断的] 间接证据:Baker 至少住独栋房,有大厨房 + 可用 sous vide 设备
- **未发现**: Sophie / 妻子 / 11 岁儿子 / 1 岁女儿具体名字 (R15 0 命中,延续 R14 留白)

**[她说过的]** 2024-12-09 3 分钟牛排烹饪视频 (`20241209001.md`):

- 公开刀具品牌: "got a nice knife from flors to table"

**[她说过的]** 2025-01-01 David Attenborough (假借 AI 配音) "Happy New Year" (`20250101001.md`):
- 完整推广 regenerative agriculture 叙事: 牛 = 草场园丁 / 碳汇

→ **R15 全新个人生活**:仅"house guests 4 口"为新数据,Sophie 名字、孩子姓名持续留白。烹饪设备具体型号首次公开 (Ooni, flors to table)。

#### 发现 9: 临床/科学争论处理策略——从"忽略"转"公开对峙 + 复述"

**[她说过的]** 2024-10-06 强纽约事件 + 2024-10-07 IBD 续篇共同呈现 R15 全新策略:
- R01-R14: Baker 几乎不公开处理外部失败案例 / 记者质疑 (R14 唯一例外 Kelly Hogan + Dr Phil 心脏事件)
- R15 两次长视频(~12 分钟 + ~3 分钟)完整复述对峙,详尽到引述具体语句("how dare you", "I know your type", "I'm getting rich off books")
- 自辩策略: 不否认自己写书赚钱,但强调 "I've made less money on my book than I would have made in 6 month if I had continued practicing medicine" (5 年前出版的书,反复被引)
- 攻击对手逻辑: "she literally is saying that diet has had a role in her symptoms but she maybe she doesn't like the concept of Aon di I don't know"
- 用患者当场反向背书: "5 minutes after that someone came up to me hey Dr Baker it's good to meet you oh by the way I put my Crohn's disease in remission"
- 用"百万人可能受益"框架: "if 50% of them could avoid lifelong medications... 40% of Chron's patients end up losing some part of their intestine... maybe we could prevent half a million people from doing that isn't it worth it"

→ **R15 全新决策**:从"沉默应对"到"完整复述 + 道德框架反驳"。这与 RFK 任命后"攻击传统医师 cowardice"形成连贯升级。

### R15 关键解读

1. **政治转向**:R14 的"irrespective of who gets an office" 在 R15 翻转为"明确投票 Trump + 公开支持 RFK 任 HHS + 攻击传统医师 cowardice"。这是 **R15 最重要的人格转变**。
2. **Rivero 兑现**:从 R13-R14 抽象"taking applications" 到 R15 公开 4 州 + waiting list 数千 + 招医师。Rivero 已从"理想"变成"可验证的运营实体"。
3. **临床研究从"发表"到"立法筹款"**:R14 完结于 case series,R15 完结于 Oklahoma 立法 + PR Pro intervention trial 提案 + $2M 预算。
4. **运动目标数据化**:R14 模糊"目标扣篮" → R15 给出体重 260→240、垂直 24"、时间 2 年。
5. **个人生活持续静默**:Sophie 名字、孩子姓名、父亲母亲姓名依然 0 命中。R15 唯一新增家庭线是"house guests 4 口"(客人不是自家)。
6. **Operation Dunk / Highland Games 静默**:R13 升 "functional tendon age" + Matt Watson,R14-R15 共 0 命中,可能项目暂停或转入私下。
7. **MeatRx 完全消失**:R01-R13 多次提及,R14-R15 0 命中,被 Rivero 商业叙事完全替代。

## 轮 16/17 (2025-01-07 → 2026-02-03)

### 调研方法

- 范围:`@ShawnBakerMD/20250107003.md` 到 `20260203001.md`,共 100 文件
- 关键 grep 命令 + 命中数:
  - `I did\|I decided\|I started\|I wrote\|my book\|I quit\|I changed\|I stopped\|I was\|became\|career\|orthopedic\|surgery\|rivero\|clinic\|company\|MeatRx\|launched` → 30 命中
  - `personal\|family\|wife\|kid\|life\|story\|journey\|son\|father\|dad\|mom\|mother\|girlfriend\|wife\|married\|wedding\|divorce\|children\|doggo\|sophie` → 30 命中
  - `rowing\|world record\|athletic\|row\|race\|marathon\|triathlon\|basketball\|track\|jiu jitsu\|BJJ\|deadlift\|squat\|trap bar\|neck\|disc\|injury\|stem cell\|hunt\|spear\|ranch\|600\|dunk\|highland\|medball\|cooper test\|farmer\|carries\|operation dunk\|plyometric\|matt watson\|eddie hall\|500m\|100m` → 30 命中
  - `Rivero\|rivero\|rivera\.com` → 20 命中
  - `RFK\|RFK Jr\|Kennedy\|HHS\|Trump\|Make America\|MAHA` → 12 命中
  - `clinic\|clinical trial\|study\|research\|TMAO\|atherosclerosis\|peer.review` → 20 命中
- 阅读文件数:15 个 (`20250107003, 20250108001, 20250111001, 20250114001, 20250118001, 20250119001, 20250119002, 20250122001, 20250127001, 20250128001, 20250214001, 20250219001, 20250223001, 20250225001, 20250917001, 20250924002, 20251004001, 20251021001, 20251029001, 20260110001, 20260113001, 20260121001`)

### R16 关键解读

1. **Rivero 从"运营"到"全美覆盖 + 众筹"**:R15 仅 4 州 + waiting list 数千 → R16 全 50 州 + Republic 众筹开放。Rivero 已从医疗服务公司升级为可投资的"运动"。
2. **MAHA 由"热望"转"清醒悲观"**:R15 Baker 刚投票 Trump + 力挺 RFK → R16 RFK 确认后 1 年,Baker 评估"上行战争"+ WV 食品色素案 2028 生效 + 被法官冻结,自承"不指望政府"而转向个人主权。这是 **R15-R16 最重要的态度反转**。
3. **临床研究从"个案 + 立法"到"主动呼吁 RCT 化"**:R15 在做 PR Pro 试验,R16 Baker 公开主张 carnivore RCT(在 2025-02 视频中)并批判植物补剂无证据。这是 **研究立场的范式升级**。
4. **运动目标数据化 + 自我年龄披露**:R15 仅"扣篮 2 年计划" → R16 给出完整 sprint 训练体系 + 自报 58 岁 + 6 周前腘绳肌伤 + 10m fly 1.53s (80%) 速度。R16 运动档案最完整。
5. **Eat Lancet 公敌名单 + 反 Eat Lancet 2.0 行动**:R15 仅"提 Eat Lancet 1.0" → R16 Baker 正式被 Eat Lancet 委员会列公敌 + Baker 主动推 Republic 众筹作为反击资源。这是 **新出现的政治化升级**。
6. **R16 新增政治盟友网络**:Ben Bikman (顾问组) + Nina Teicholz (幕后功臣) + Lex Fridman 跨栏联袂。R16 Baker 在 carnivore 圈政治位置显化。
7. **牛肉供应国家安全隐患 (新议题)**:R16 Baker 首次把美牛萎缩上升到 "national security" 高度,推动 MCOOL 标签 + Pack-Stockyards 法执法。这是 **R01-R15 0 命中的新政策线**。
8. **2025 饮食指南 (新事件)**:RFK + USDA Rollins 签名,蛋白质 RDA 翻倍到 1.2-1.6 g/kg,糖上限 10%→2%,但保留 10% 饱和脂肪上限 — Baker 评分"big win 但不彻底"。这是 **R16 最重要的政府事件**。
9. **个人生活持续静默**:Sophie 名字、孩子姓名、父亲母亲姓名 0 命中。R16 唯一新增家庭线是"house guests 4 口"(R15 已提)+ 20260121 提"walking my dogs"在长陡 driveway。
10. **Operation Dunk / Highland Games 静默延续**:R13 公开,R14-R16 0 命中,可能转入私下或已搁置。
11. **MeatRx 完全消失**:R01-R13 多次提及,R14-R16 0 命中,被 Rivero 商业叙事完全替代。

## 轮 17/17 (2026-02-08 → 2026-05-29)

**范围**:`@ShawnBakerMD/` 文件 1601-1625(`20260208001.md` → `20260529001.md`),共 24 个 `.md` + 1 个 `manifest.yaml`(共 25 个文件)。
**方法**:逐条 `ls | sort | sed -n '1601,1625p'` 确认范围;运行 5 套 grep(决策关键词 / 个人关键词 / 运动关键词 / 商业+主题关键词 / 目标人物 Peptide/Enhanced Games/Crohn);每个文件最多 Read 1 次。

### R17 grep 命令与命中数

| 命令 | 命中文件数 |
|---|---|
| `grep -l -E 'I did\|I decided\|I started\|I wrote\|my book\|I quit\|I changed\|I stopped\|I was\|became\|career\|orthopedic\|surgery\|rivero\|clinic\|company\|MeatRx\|launched'` | 15 / 24 |
| `grep -l -E 'personal\|family\|wife\|kid\|son\|father\|dad\|mom\|mother\|girlfriend\|married\|wedding\|divorce\|children\|doggo\|sophie'` | 22 / 24 |
| `grep -l -E 'rowing\|world record\|row\|ran\|race\|racing\|marathon\|triathlon\|baskteball\|track\|weight\|jiu jitsu\|BJJ\|deadlift\|squat\|trap bar\|neck\|disc\|injury\|stem cell\|hunt\|spear\|ranch\|600\|dunk\|highland\|medball\|cooper test\|farmer\|carries\|operation dunk\|plyometric\|matt watson\|eddie hall\|500m\|100m\|40 yard\|fastest'` | 23 / 24 |
| `grep -l -E 'rivero\|Rivero\|MeatRx\|book\|peptides\|Peptides\|Enhanced\|Mr. Beast\|Crohn\|Operation Dunk\|What The Hell'` | 10 / 24 |
| `grep -l -E 'Peptide\|peptide\|BPC\|TB500\|epitalon\|GHK\|Enhanced Games\|Beast\|Crohn\|new book\|book update\|Operation dunk\|cooper test\|marry\|wife\|son\|father\|ranch\|Matt Watson\|Eddie Hall\|steak and butter\|mom\|dad'` | 22 / 24 |

So I need to lose a little bit of weight, probably about 10 lbs." 目标减到 ~238。R16 6'5" 250 lbs → R17 6'5" 248 lbs,延续 60 岁前的减脂目标。**体重轨迹缓慢下行**。

Baker 明确把 Rivero 服务拆解为:① 医生(provider)② 教练(coach)③ App 数据反馈三件套。**R17 商业化已从"问诊"升级为"App 订阅 + 健康教练 + 医生监督"复合 SaaS**。

### R17 关键解读

1. **R17 三大新出现维度**:
   - **家庭祖源信息首次出现** — 母亲来自南非、1988 在 University of Texas 读生物学位、24-25 岁入伍。R01-R16 全部 0 命中。
   - **个人英雄叙事扩张** — "前医生时代"救人故事(Saving someone's life 视频),把叙事半径从"外科医生"扩到"生物学大学生"。
   - **国际旅行计划公开** — 2026 计划首次去澳大利亚(食肉友好国家选题驱动)。

2. **Rivero 商业模式定型**:R17 Rivero 已经形成 "医生 + 健康教练 + Revere App + 数据反馈" 的复合 SaaS,Baker 每周投入 1-2 个时段与病人视频会议。R16 是"全美覆盖 + 众筹",R17 是"标准化产品形态 + 真实病人证言"。

3. **Baker 的"反 Longevity Industrial Complex"立场公开化**:R17 Baker 公开反对 Bryan Johnson($1M 项目) + Dave Asprey(300 补剂) + 培植肉(Upside Foods) + Enhanced Games(Peter Thiel)。**这是 R17 最显著的反主流方向感 — Baker 现在是有立场的,而非"只讲肉"**。

4. **MAHA 内部立场分裂**:R17 Baker 仍力挺 RFK(schizophrenia 治愈声明),但对 Trump 的"草甘膦 = national security"持中性偏中立的"中间立场"。**这是 R16 评估"上行战争"后,Baker 进一步细化的政治站位**。

5. **运动档案进入"多项目世界级"状态**:R17 Baker 自报 ~60 岁,世界级成绩包括 rowing world record holder + Olympic lifting world-class + sprinting world-class + jumping world-class + 18.5-19 mph 跑速。**R16 报"58 岁" → R17 报"接近 60 岁" + 即将进入 masters 田径**。

6. **个人实验公开化**:R17 Baker 把 30 天 carb 重新引入实验作为 YouTube 主题内容发布,公开减重 10 lb / 增炎症 / 二头肌腱炎 → 恢复 carnivore 5 lb / 腱炎消失。**R17 内容从"我推荐 carnivore"变成"我试过不是 carnivore,这就是证据"**。

7. **R17 0 命中清单(可能仍静默)**:
   - 新书状态:R17 0 命中"new book" / "book 2" / "writing"。R11-R12 MeatRx Cookbook 之后无新书宣告。
   - 妻子 / 孩子姓名:延续 R01-R16 0 命中(只提"my wife" + "my son")。
   - Sophie(可能的宠物名):R17 0 命中。
   - Belize / New Zealand 出生地:R17 0 命中。
   - 具体椎间盘 / 颈部诊断名:R17 0 命中(只提"back and neck issues from accidents" + "nerve damage")。
   - Cooper test 12-min run 重测:R17 0 命中。
   - BJJ 蓝带进展:R17 0 命中。
   - Operation Dunk 进展:R17 0 命中(可能已搁置)。
   - Matt Watson / Eddie Hall / 500m 等合作:R17 0 命中(对应 R16 0 命中)。
   - Peptides(BPC-157 / TB-500 等具体品牌):R17 0 命中(Baker 提到 Kenton Brown "probably on TRT and all these peptides" 但 Baker 自己的 peptide 立场 0 命中)。
   - Mr. Beast Crohn's 治疗:R17 0 命中(只讨论了 Mr. Beast 的 Future Chicken 视频,未提 Crohn's)。
   - 1988 救人故事中的具体人物姓名:R17 0 命中(只说"this is a story I want to share")。
   - Baker 的"医学失败"故事:R17 0 命中(R01-R16 反复 0 命中)。

8. **R17 与 R16 决策变化对比**:
   | 维度 | R16 (2025-01 → 2026-02) | R17 (2026-02 → 2026-05) | 变化 |
   |---|---|---|---|
   | Rivero | 全美 50 州 + Republic 众筹 | Revere App + coach + 病人证言 + 双周视频 | 从"地域扩张"到"产品定型" |
   | MAHA 立场 | "上行战争"+ 不指望政府 | 仍力挺 RFK + 中立评论 Trump 草甘膦 | 内部立场细分 |
   | 运动目标 | sprint 训练体系 + 10m fly 1.53s | 18.5-19 mph 跑速 + Olympic lifting + masters 田径宣告 | 范围扩张 |
   | 反 Longevity Industrial Complex | 反对 plant supplements 无证据 | 公开反对 Bryan Johnson + Dave Asprey + Enhanced Games | 升级为"具体点名" |
   | 个人生活 | 匿名 | 1988 救人故事 + 母亲南非 + 24-25 入伍 | 首次披露 |
   | 体重 | 245-250 lbs | 248 lbs + 目标 238 lbs | 缓慢下行 |
   | 年龄披露 | 自报 58 | 自报 ~60 / 接近 60 | 临近 60 |
   | 临床研究 | 公开呼吁 carnivore RCT | 病人证言 + 3 个月数据(非 RCT 路径) | 路径分歧 |
   | 妻子 / 孩子 | 匿名 | 仍匿名(只提 wife + son) | 持续匿名 |
   | MeatRx | 0 命中 | 0 命中 | 商业叙事已完全替代 |
   | Operation Dunk | 0 命中 | 0 命中 | 可能已搁置 |
   | 新书 | 0 命中 | 0 命中 | 静默 |
   | 1988 救人故事 | 0 命中 | 出现 | 新增 |
   | Enhanced Games | 0 命中 | 出现 + 1996 USA Powerlifting 立场 | 新增 |
   | Bryan Johnson 反对 | 0 命中 | 出现 | 新增 |
   | 培植肉(Upside Foods) | 0 命中 | 出现 | 新增 |

9. **R17 Baker 的"60 岁规划"轮廓已清晰**:减脂到 ~238 lbs + 进入 masters 田径 + Rivero 持续运营 + 公开反对 longevity 工业 + 国际演讲(澳大利亚 2026)。**这是 R17 给出的最完整"60 岁 baker"画像**。
